Catalog number : M00343
Uniform title: sādhanamālā Vol. 2
Author : bhattacharya benoytosh
Description:
This text is a collection of sādhanas, that is, procedures for the worship of Buddhist Tantric deities especially those of the Anuttarayoga Tantras. A description of the deity, its form and colour, vehicle etc along with its related visualization is supplied in each sādhana together with that deitys Mantra and its applications in worship and yogic practice. They are composed by a large range of Buddhist teachers many of eminence. The earliest is the Siddha Saraha who is dated to around 630 CE. Along with him are a number of other great Buddhist Siddhas, such as Nāgārjuna, Kukkuripa, Indrabhūti, Kṛṣṇapāda, śabara and others who belonged to the 7th and 8th century. The last contributors belong to the 12th century which gives us a lower limit for the date of the collection.
Notes:
Data entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the direction of Mark S. T. Dyczkowski.

Revision 0: February 21, 2015
Publisher : Baroda Oriental Institute
Publication year : 1925
Publication city : Baroda
Publication country : India

sādhanamālā |

dvitīyo bhāgaḥ |

171.

namastārodbhavakurukullāyai |

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī kvacinmanohare sthāne mṛdumasūrakādyupaviṣṭaḥ 
paṭādigatāṃ bhagavatīmavatārya bāhyapūjāṃ kṛtvā svahṛdi nirmalaṃ 
pūrṇacandrābhamādisvarasamudbhavaṃ candraṃ vibhāvya bījaṃ tasyopari [k: 
yasyo] nyaset |

ūṣmāṇāṃ ca caturthaṃ tu agnivarṇopari sthitam |
īkāreṇa [k: ikā] samāyuktamākāśadvayabhūṣitam ||

tasya citramayūkhābhiḥ kṛtvā nirmalinaṃ [k, g: nirmalaṃ] jagat |
sahādidhātukaṃ śodhya kurukullaparvate [k; pūrṇe] gatām ||

sañcodya ca tathā tārāmānayitvā puraścaret |
tasmād bījāt mahāpūjāmeghāṃśca sphārayed budhaḥ ||

puṣpadhūpatathādīpagandhanaivedyasañcayaiḥ |
lāsyamālyanṛtyagītavādyapūjādibhistathā ||

p. 344)

ratnatrayaṃ me śaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ pratidiśāmyagham |
anumode jagatpuṇyaṃ buddhabodhau dadhe manaḥ |
iti mantraṃ tridhā vācyaṃ tataḥ [k: omits] kṣantavyamityapi [k: i] ||

tatreyaṃ gāthā - 

yat kṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ [k: omits ] kiñcit mayā mūḍhadhiyā punaḥ |
kṣantavyaṃ tat tvayā devi yatastrātā'si dehinām ||

cittaṃ maitrī vihāre ca niveṣṭavyaṃ punastadā |
karuṇācittamutpāṃdya pramodacittamāvahet ||

paścādupekṣate sarvaṃ cittamātravyavasthayā |
cittaṃ śūnyaṃ tataḥ kuryāt prakṛtākārahānaye ||

śūnyatāvahninā [k: tāhninā] dagdhāḥ pañcaskandhāḥ punarbhavāḥ |
paṭhitvā oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham ||

muhūrttaṃ śūnyatāyogaṃ kuryāt cittaya viśramam |
pratijñāṃ prāktanīṃ smṛtvā bījamātraṃ punaḥ smaret ||

pratāritā mayā sattvā ekāntaparinirvṛtāḥ |
kathaṃ tān uddhariṣyāmi agādhādaṃ bhavasāgarāt ||

iti sattvakṛpāviṣṭo niśceṣṭāṃ śūnyatāṃ tyajet |
dharmadhātumayaṃ cittamutpādayati cetasā ||

buddhvādhiṣṭhānato bījamutpalākhyaṃ tato bhavet |
utpale candrabimbaṃ tu akārasvarasambhavam [k, g: tāṃkā] ||

tasmiṃścandre punarbījaṃn tasmād gabhastayo gatāḥ |
tābhirviśodhitā [k, g: abhinive] dhyeyā niḥśeṣā lokadhātavaḥ ||

p. 345)

śodhyaṃ bodhyaṃ tathā sarvaṃ raśmibhirbuddhakoṭayaḥ |
viśatastānutpale [k, g: nirgata] dhyāyāt tatastārodayo bhavet ||

caturbhujāṃ raktavarṇāṃ raktapadmāsanasthitām |
raktavastrayugāṃ bhavyāṃ raktatāṭaṅkamaulikām ||

savyabhujābhyāmabhayaṃ śaraṃ ca dadhatīṃ punaḥ |
avasavyadvitaye cāparaktotpaladhāriṇīm ||

kurukullādriguhāntasthāmārolikamaulikām [k, g: llādigrahā ; k: mālo] |
raktapadmāsanādhastāt rāhustasyopari [k, g: rāsabhasyo] sthitaḥ ||

kāmadevaḥ sapatnīko bhāvanīyo'tivihvalaḥ |
rāhorupari sapatnīkakāmadevasthitāsanām |
vajraparyaṅkinīṃ tatra sarvacitrakalāvatīm ||

dhyātvā bhagavatīṃ samyak sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām |
samayamūrtiṃ samāsādya jñānacakraṃ samāhvayet ||

jhaṭityākāraniṣpannaṃ jñānacakraṃ puraḥsthitam |
jaḥ hūṁ vaṁ horityānenākṛṣya praveśya baddhvā toṣayet ||

samayamudrāprayogeṇa sukhamārge [k, g: mukha] niveśayet |
pāṇibhyāmañjaliṃ kṛtvā māṃsalau [k, ḍh: lairnamito] nāmitau 
yadā ||

dīrghābhyāṃ [ḍh: dīpābhyāṃ] tu kṛtā sūciḥ sūcimadhye [k, g: 
omit sūci] tvanāmike |
latābhyāṃ madhyamau śliṣṭau dvāvaṅguṣṭhāvadhogatau ||

anena bandhayet samayonayā jñānacakraṃ praveśayet |
tato'bhiṣekaṃ prārthayet buddhāniti [the ḍ, dh, dh: add gāthāṃ after 
this, which makes the metre faulty] paṭhan kṛtī ||

p. 346)

bodhivajreṇa buddhānāṃ yathā datto mahāmahaḥ |
mamāpi cāṇanārthāya khavajrādyaṃ dadāhi me ||

te dadanti mahadbhūtā rājyābhiṣekanāmataḥ [k: mahyā, g: sahyā] |
puṣpābhiṣekavatprājñaḥ pañcabhirlocanādibhiḥ ||

abhiṣekaṃ mahāvajraṃ traidhātukanamaskṛtam |
dadāmi sarvabuddhānāṃ triguhyālayasambhavam ||

buddhābhiṣekatastārādharmacūḍāmaṇirbhavet |
raktacittaprabhābhābhirbhāsayantī [k, g: rbhrama] jagattrayam ||

prāptābhiṣekaratnastu sarvasiddhiṃ prasādhayet |
varṣārdhaṃ ca dṛḍhāveśāt yathoktavidhinā puraḥ ||

trisandhyāsu baliṃ dattvā bhāvanākramapūrvakaḥ |
tārāhṛdayenānena japet mantraṃ samāhitaḥ ||

oṃ kurukulle hrīḥ [k: hrīṁ] hūṁ svāhā | tataḥ pūrvasevāmakṣaralakṣaṃ japtvā 
paścāt karma samārabhet |

saṃśena [k, g: aṅge] varddhate prajñā aparāṃśena vaśyakṛt |
aṃśenaiva tṛtīyena viṣākarṣī [k, g: karṣaṃ] bhavennaraḥ ||

sarvasya lokasya bhavet sa pūjyaḥ [k: sampuṭā] strīṇāṃ madenāpi hi 
garvitānām [k, g: garbhiṇīnāṃ] |
yathecchayā mantrī [ḍh, g, kh: mantradharo] varo vibhuṅkte 
abhyāsayogāt surasundarīṇām ||

p. 347)

hrīḥkāraṃ madanātapatranihitaṃ strīṇāṃ bhavet srāvakaṃ jihvāyāṃ ca tadeva 
buddhijananaṃ hṛnmadhyake caiva tat |
daṣṭānāṃ paribhāvitaṃ viṣaharaṃ dharmākṣaraṃ svakṣaraṃ tārāyā hṛdayaṃ 
trilokavijayi jñeyaṃ kṛpāśālibhiḥ ||

kurukullāṃ vibhāvya svāṃ tārodbhūtāṃ [ḍh: svabhāvo] saroruhe |
sādhyamantargataṃ kṛtvā bhāvayet kāmavihvalām ||

jñānasattvābjapīyūṣadharāṃ tanmūrdhni cintayet |
patantīmiti saptāhaṃ trisandhyaṃ bhāvayet kṛtī ||

kramābhyāsāt kṣudrasiddhiṃ sādhayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ |
sādhayecca mahāsiddhiṃ sarvathā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

|| kalpoktatārodbhavakurukullāsādhanaṃ samāptam ||

172.

namastārodbhavakurukullāyai [k, b: adds kalpāmnāyoktakramāya before 
kuru] |

yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa sadevāsuramānuṣāḥ |
dhruvaṃ kiṅkaratāṃ yānti tāṃ namasyāmi tāriṇīm ||

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī kvacinmano'nukūle [kh, a: nmanoharestulye] sthāne 
strījanasaṃsargādirahite [k, b: tena] sukhāsīnaḥ [kh, b: 
sukhāsanā] svahṛdi prathamasvarapariṇatacandramaṇḍalopari 
saraśmikahrīḥkārabījavinirgataraktamayūkhasamūhairjagadavabhāsanapūrvakaṃ 
[k, b: kāraniḥsṛta]  kurukullaparvatasthitakurukullāṃ [k, b: omits 
llaparvata sañcitya] 

p. 348)

bhagavatīṃ sañcodyānīya purato [k, ḍh, g, kh, a: puro] 
gaganadeśe [k, ḍh, g, k, b: gamana] sañcintya 
hṛdbījaraśminirmitavividhapuṣpadhūpādipūjābhiḥ pūjayet | tadanu vandanā 
pāpadeśanā puṇyānumodanā pariṇāmanā triśaraṇagamanaṃ bodhicittotpādapūrvakam 
[k, b, kh, a: dana] - 

ratnatrayaṃ me śaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ pratidiśāmyagham |
animode jagatpuṇyaṃ buddhabodhau dadhe manaḥ ||

iti tridhā paṭhet | tataścaturbrahmavihārabhāvanāpūrvakaṃ sarvadharmaśūnyatāṃ 
vibhāvya tadadhiṣṭhānamantraṃ paṭhet - oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | tadanu sakalaṃ jagat duḥkhitaṃ 
karuṇayā'valambya svacittaṃ bījarūpamavalokya tatpariṇataṃ raktakuvalayopari 
prathamasvarapariṇatacandrasthitabījaraśmibhiḥ pūrvavat 
jagadavabhāsyānantatathāgatakoṭīḥ sañcodyānīyāntarbhāvayet | tadutpalādipariṇatāṃ 
raktavarṇāṃ raktapadmacandrāsanāṃ raktapaṭṭāṃśukottarīyāṃ raktaprabhāvalayāṃ 
raktatāṭaṅkakirīṭinīṃ [kh, a: lāṭa] caturbhujāṃ savye abhayapradāṃ 
dvitīye samāpūritaśarāṃ vāme ratnacāpadharāṃ dvitīye raktotpaladharāṃ 
amitābhatathāgatamukuṭāṃ [k, b: ṭinaṃ] 
kurukullaparvataguhāsthitarāhumastakasthitasapatnīkakāmadevoparisthitāṃ [k, b: 
kullā] śṛṅgārarasānvitaprathamayauvanopetāṃ [kh, a: rasojjvala, 
k, b: rasojvana] kurukullāṃ bhāvayet | evaṃ [k, g: omit evaṃ 
rbhāvayet] samayasattvaṃ niṣpādya jhaṭiti jñānasattvaṃ purataḥ samānīya 

p. 349)

sampūjya samayamudrayā [k, b: mudrā] sukhamārgeṇa antarbhāvayet | 
tatreyaṃ [k, b: tatraivaṃ] samayamudrā - 
sampuṭāñjalermadhyamāsūcimadhye anāmike praveśya madhyamāpṛṣṭhe latādvayaṃ 
saṃsthitaṃ dvāvaṅguṣṭhāvadhaḥ praveśyotpalamudrā tadākārā [ḍh, g, 
kh, kh, a: omit] | tadanu - 

bodhivajreṇa buddhānāṃ yathā datto mahāmahaḥ |
mamāpi trāṇanārthāya khavajrādyaṃ dadāhi me ||

iti prārthya - 

abhiṣekaṃ mahāvajraṃ traidhātukanamaskṛtam |
dadāmi sarvabuddhānāṃ triguhyālayasambhavam ||

iti paṭhantībhirbuddhājñayā locanādibhirabhiṣekaṃ dīyamānaṃ dhyāyāt | makuṭe 
amitābho vyavasthitaḥ | evaṃ bhāvanāṃ kṛtvā japaṃ kuryāt na drutaṃ na vilambitaṃ 
nāspaṣṭaṃ na mātrāhīnaṃ asatsaṅkalpavarjitam - oṃ kurukulle hrīḥ [k, g: 
hrīṁ svāhā, ḍh: hrīḥ huḥ svāhā] hūṁ svāhā | evaṃ trisandhyakrameṇa 
piṣṭakaśarkarādibalidānapuraḥsaraṃ [k, ḍh, g, kh, a: piṣṭa] 
sādaranirantaradṛḍhāveśena [k, b: omits sādara mayācitaṃ ; k, g: 
sāvara] ṣaṇmāsān bhāvayet siddhinimittāni bhavanti | tadanu prathame māse 
rogādyupadravaśamaṃ bhavati dvitīyamāse bhojanaṃ praṇītamayācitam tṛtīye 
nānāvidhavastralābhaḥ caturthe tāmbūlaṃ nānāvidhamanavarataṃ labhet pañcame 
vicitrarūpādiguṇasamuditastrīsaṅgamaḥ [k, ḍh, g, kh, a: 
saṅgaḥ] ṣaṣṭhe māse sadevanāgayakṣagandharvakinnarādidivyastrīsaṅgamaḥ |

p. 350)

prāyeṇa bhagavatīṃ bhāvayataḥ [k, b: yet] strīṇāmevopadravo bhavati | 
tadetāni mārakarmāṇi parityajya bhāvayato bhagavatyāḥ svapnesākṣāt vā pratyādeśo 
bhavati | khaḍgāñjanapādalepāntarddhānarasarasāyanakhecarabhūcarapātālasiddhi##-
siddhīḥ sādhayet | yadi pratyādeśo na bhavati tadā pūrvoktamudrāṃ baddhvā 
pūjābalidānādipūrvakaṃ ṣaṇmāsānte pūrnamāsyāṃ sakalāṃ rātriṃ japet | tato jvalati 
siddhaśca bhavati | tayā mudrayā brahmendrarudranārāyaṇaprabhṛtayaḥ [k, 
ḍh, g, kh, a: brahmopendra] samākṛṣṭāḥ samāgamya 
kiṅkaratāmupagamya sādhakābhilaṣitaṃ sampādayanti | tataḥ prabhṛti 
janmajarāmaraṇarahitaḥ siddho lokadhātūn [k, ḍh, g, kh, a: 
dhātuṃ] gatvā tathāgatān paśyati bhūmidhāraṇyādikaṃ prāpnoti |

|| muktakena tārodbhavakurukullāsādhanaṃ [k, b: āryatārākalpakrameṇa 
kurukullā] samāptam ||

173.

namaḥ kurukullāyai |

ādau mantrī sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ svahṛdbījaraśmibhiragrato bhagavatīṃ 
buddhabodhisattvāṃścanīya sampūjya triśaraṇagamanādikaṃ [k: omits gamana] 
kuryāt | tataḥ śūnyatāmadhimucya mantreṇādhiṣṭhāya

p. 351)

akārasambhavacandramaṇḍale tāṁkāraṃ [kh: aṁkā] raśmimālinaṃ vicintya 
tatpariṇatāṃ kurukullāṃ bhagavatīṃ paśyet raktāṃ raktāṣṭadalapadmasūryāsane 
[kh, k, b: omit raktāṣṭadala] vajraparyaṅkaniṣaṇāṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ 
savyāvasavyaprathamabhujābhyāṃ trailokyavijayamudrādharāṃ 
dvitīyadakṣiṇavāmakarābhyāṃ aṅkuśaraktotpaladharāṃ 
pariśiṣṭabhujadvayenākarṇapūritadhanuḥśarāṃ raktāmbaradharāṃ [kh: omits] 
pañcatathāgatamakuṭiṃ sādhyasya hṛdi raktotpalavaraṭake candramaṇḍale raktavarṇaṃ 
tāṁkāraṃ [kh: aṁkā] vibhāvya vāyumaṇḍalārūḍhaṃ taṃ hṛdyaṅkuśena 
viddhvāṅīya hṛdaye tasyopaviśya raktotpalakalikāśareṇa sādhyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ 
vidhyan mantraṃ japet | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ - oṃ kurukulle hrīḥ [ḍh: krīḥ] 
amukaṃ me vaśamānaya hoḥ svāhā | tatastaṃ vihvalaṃ vaśībhūtaṃ pādayornipatitaṃ 
dṛṣṭvā preṣayet | evaṃ śakramapi vaśamānayati |

|| iti ṣaḍbhujakurukullāsādhanam [k, b: omits ṣaḍbhuja] ||

174.

namaḥ kurukullāyai |

pūrvavat śūnyatāparyantaṃ vibhāvya repheṇa pariṇatasūrye [kh, a: 
sūryamaṇḍale] hrīḥkāraṃ sphārasaṃhāravigrahaṃ [k, b: 
sphuraṇasaṃharaṇa] ca dhyātvā jhaṭiti [ḍh, k, b: jhagiti] 
tatpariṇāmataḥ kurukullāṃ bhagavatīṃ aṣṭabhujāṃ raktavarṇāṃ 
raktāṣṭadalapadmasūrye vajraparyaṅkaniṣaṇāṃ kūṭāgāramadhyanivāsinīṃ 
[kh, a: madhye] 

p. 352)

prathamakaradvayena trailokyavijayamudrādharāṃ avaśiṣṭadakṣiṇakaraiḥ aṅkuśaṃ 
ākarṇapūritaśaraṃ varadamudrāṃ dadhānāṃ pariśiṣṭavāmabhujaiḥ pāśaṃ cāpaṃ 
utpalaṃ dadhānāṃ sakalālaṅkāravatīṃ bhāvayet | pūrvadale prasannatārāṃ dakṣiṇadale 
niṣpannatārāṃ paścimadale jayatārāṃ uttaradale karṇatārāṃ aiśānadale cundāṃ 
[k: cūndrāṃ] āgneyadale aparājitāṃ nairṛtyadale pradīpatārāṃ vāyavyadale 
[kh, a: vāyavye] gaurītārāṃ ca dhyāyāt | etāśca sarvā raktavarṇāḥ 
pañcatathāgatamukuṭā [kh: ṭinyaḥ] vajraparyaṅkaniṣaṇā 
dakṣiṇabhujābhyāṃ varadamudrā''karṇapūritaśaradharā vāmabhujābhyāṃ 
utpalacāpadharāḥ | pūrvadvāre vajravetālīṃ lambodarāṃ vikṛtamukhīṃ raktavarṇāṃ 
[k: omits] akṣobhyamukuṭāṃ dakṣiṇahastābhyāṃ tarjanyaṅkuśadharāṃ 
vāmakarābhyāṃ vajraghaṇṭāpāśadharām dakṣiṇadvāre aparājitāṃ pītavarṇāṃ 
ratnasambhavamukuṭāṃ dakṣiṇahastābhyāṃ danḍāṅkuśadharāṃ vāmahastābhyāṃ 
ghaṇṭāpāśadharām paścimadvāre ekajaṭāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ ūrdhvakeśāṃ 
lambodarāṃ dantāvaṣṭabdhauṣṭhāṃ amitābhamukuṭāṃ dakṣiṇakarābhyāṃ 
vajrāṅkuśadharāṃ vāmakarābhyāṃ ghaṇṭāpāśadharām uttaradvāre vajragāndhārīṃ 
[k, g, k, b: gandhā] kanakaśyāmāṃ amoghasiddhimukuṭāṃ 
vikṛtamukhīṃ lambodarāṃ dakṣiṇabhujābhyāṃ khaḍgāṅkuśadharāṃ [k, b: 
khaṭvāṅgā] vāmabhujābhyāṃ ghaṇṭāpāśadharāṃ cintayet | etāścatasra 
ālīḍhapadasthāḥ | tatreyaṃ trailokyavijayamudrā - talau pṛṣṭhalagnau 
anāmikādvayaṃ granthiṃ kṛtvā kaniṣṭhāṃ madhyamāṅguṣṭhenāvaṣṭabhya 
tarjanyau 

p. 353)

aṅkuśākāreṇa salīlaṃ lalāṭadeśe dhārayet | bhāvanākhinno mantraṃ japet oṃ 
kurukulle hrīḥ [k, ḍh, g: add hūṁ after this] svāhā | ayutajāpena sarve 
janā vaśyā bhavanti niyutena [ḍh: triyu] mantriṇaḥ lakṣeṇa rājānaḥ | utpalaṃ 
ghṛtamadhvāktaṃ aśvatthasamidhaśca [k, g: āśvatha, kh, a: 
aśvatthena] juhūyāt iṣṭaṃ padaṃ prāpnoti | śrīphalakāṣṭhenāgniṃ prajvālya 
ghṛtamadhvāktānāṃ śrīphalānāṃ [kh: omits] lakṣaṃ juhūyāt [kh: 
japet] rājamantriṇo vaśyā bhavanti iti | utpalasahasraṃ juhūyāt 
ghṛtapradīpasahasraṃ devyā agrato nivedayet | sarve rājāno vaśyā bhavantīti |

|| kṛtiriyamindrabhūtipādānām ||

175.

atha mantrī strīpuruṣau vaśīkartukāmaḥ śuklapratipadamārabhya trisandhyamayutamekaṃ 
pratyahaṃ japet bhagavatīmavalambya raktavastraṃ paridhāya gātraṃ ca prāvṛtya | 
tatrāyaṃ kramaḥ - svahṛdi ravistharaktahrīḥkārajaraktāṣṭadalakamalaṃ 
yoniparyantagatanālaṃ vicintya taddaleṣvaṣṭasu [k: dalakamalānaṣṭau]

ḍh, g: aṃ aḥ] ityaṣṭasvarapariṇatān 
atihṛṣṭapuṣṭakruddharaktavarṇānanukramasthitān svanāsikāpavanapreritān 

p. 354)

dṛṣṭvā svavāmanāmikāvivareṇa niḥsṛtya sādhyāyā

ākarṇapūritaraktotpalakalikāśaranikaraiḥ sādhyāyāḥ [k, ḍh, g, 
kh, a: sādhyā] kiñjalkaṃ viddhvā tatpariṇataśarānādāya 
tadvāmanāsikāvivareṇa niḥsṛtya yogī dakṣiṇanāsikayā [kh: kāyā] tān 
svahṛdi praviṣṭān vicintayet | paścādutpalanālena galake baddhvā yoniṃ ca viddhvā

- oṃ kurukulle hrīḥ amukīṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā | saptāhāt sidhyatīti |

|| bhramarīyogakurukullāsādhanam ||

176.

praṇamya kurukullakāṃ mātaraṃ siddhidāyinīm |
upadeśo likhyate'syāḥ sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ |
kurukullāyogayukto bhāvayedupadeśakam ||

nāsāpuṭena niścārya yaṁkārapariṇatavātamaṇḍalārūḍhaḥ [ḍh: paṁkā] 
utpalena hṛdi bhage vā''tāḍya

tamānīya vāmapārśve uttānaṃ saṃsthāpya raśmīn svahṛdi hrīḥkāreṇa [k, 
ḍh: kāre] dakṣiṇapuṭena praveśayet | paścāt punaḥ 
hṛdbījotthitaraktotpalākāraraśmirekhābhyantareṇaiva bhrūvivaramāgamya vāmakarṇaṃ 
saṃspṛśya mastakaṃ veṣṭayitvā dakṣiṇakarṇavivareṇa nirgatya tayā 
kalikāraśmirekhayā ākarṇākṛṣṭotpalakalikānālaśaraśuṣire [k, g: 
vāsaśaraṣire] praveśya kiñcit kalikāmukhaṃ vikāśya [k, g: 
vihāyāsya] sādhyasya liṅge bhage vā grasananyāyena muhurmuhuḥ vedhayet | bījeneti 
kecit | śarāghātapraviṣṭaraśminā sarvāṅge vyāptaṃ sravantaṃ vihvalaṃ aśaraṇaṃ 
paśyet | yadi utthitaṃ tāḍayet tadā svapādatale patitaṃ sādhyaṃ vicintya punastathaiva 
vedhamanuṣṭhayet | yadā tu vedhanāsamartho bhavati tadā hṛdbījarandhre [k, g:

oṃ namaḥ kurukullāyai |

atha mantrī strīpuruṣau vaśīkartukāmaḥ śuklapratipadamārabhya trisandhyamayutamekaṃ

prathamaṃ mantriṇā sarvasattvānātmānaṃ ca sarvaduḥkhebhyo vimoktukāmena 
prabhātasandhyāyāmutthāya mukhaprakṣālanādikaṃ [k: pramukha] kṛtvā

dhet] | paścāt [kh, a: omits] oṃ

prabhāsvararūpaṃ cintayet | tadanantaraṃ svahṛdaye [k, kh, a: omit]

sādhakaśarāt 

p. 384)

sādhyakarasthaśarakalikāyāṃ tadraśmivibuddhāyāṃ tathā dakṣiṇakarṇena praveśya

sarvakāryakaraṇotsukā bhavatīti | 

vidhāyāsāditaṃ puṇyaṃ kurukullākhyasādhanam |
tena sarvajanāḥ santu vaśyabodhiniyojane ||

nama āryapratisarāyai |

tasyā mahāpratisarāyāḥ pūrvasyāṃ diśi tathaiva pūrvayogamadhikṛtya 
viśvapadmadhye hūṁkāreṇa vajracihnapariṇāmena [g: bīja] 
mahāsāhasaramarddhanī kṛṣṇavarṇā piṅgalorddhvakeśā narakapālālaṅkṛtā 
bhrūbhṛkuṭidaṃṣṭrākarālavadanā sphuratsūrya maṇḍalāsanā lalitākṣepeṇa 
mahābhūtamahāyakṣānākramamāṇā kaṭakakeyūramaṇḍitā hāranūpurabhīṣitā tasyā 
dakṣiṇaprathamabhuje 

p. 407)

varadavajraṃ dvitīye aṅkuśaṃ tṛtīye śaraṃ caturthe khaḍgaṃ vāmaprathamabhuje 
tarjanīpāśaṃ dvitīye paraśuṃ tṛtīye [k, g: omits paraśuḥ tṛtīye] 
dhanuḥ caturthe padmopari ṣoḍaśaratnam tasyā mūlamukhaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dakṣiṇe śvetaṃ 
pṛṣṭhe pītaṃ vāme haritaṃ sarvaṃ trinetram nānāratnādyalaṅkṛtaśarīrā 
mahābalaparākramā raudraveśā vaṭavṛkṣopaśobhitā 
saptamātrādidevatāsantrāsanakarī revatyādigrahāṇāṃ santrāsitamanāḥ 
vāsukyādyaṣṭanāgasantrāsanakarī vātapittaśleṣmādisaṃśodhanakarī 
raudratamo'ndhakārameghasphuṭanakarī sarvāpamṛtyunivāraṇakarī | tasyā jāpamantraḥ 
- oṃ amṛtavare varapravaraviśuddhe hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ [ṭhe second phaṭ is 
omitted in k, g] svāhā |

tato mahāpratisarāyā dakṣiṇadigbhavane viśvapadmopari candramaṇḍalamadhye 
māṁkārabījapariṇāmena jhaṭiti mahāmāyūrī pītavarṇā sūryama.ḍalālīḍhā 
sattvaparyaṅkinī trimukhā trinetrā aṣṭabhujā ratnamukuṭinī sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā tasyā 
dakṣiṇaprathamabhuje varadaṃ dvitīye ratnaghaṭadharā tṛtīye cakraṃ caturthe khaḍgaṃ 
vāmaprathamabhuje pātropari bhikṣāṃkṣuḥ dvitīye mayūrapicchaṃ tṛtīye ghaṇṭopari

raktam aśokavṛkṣopaśobhitā tatpārśvasthitā [k: sthitasapta] 
sasaptaviṣasañchādanakarī saraudrakapilādirākṣasīvidhvaṃsanakarī samastanāgādīnāṃ 
santrāsanakarī devanāgayakṣagandharvairnamaskaraṇīyā 
sasaptaviṃśanakṣatrādinavagrahādibhiḥ [k, g: kṣatragrahā] sevanīyā 

p. 408)

sasthāvarajaṅgamaviṣavimocanīyā [viṣa omitted in k, g] 
sadevadaityāsurasammohanakarī | tasyā bhagavatyā jāpamantraḥ - oṃ amṛtavilokini 
garbhasaṃrakṣaṇi ākarṣaṇi [g: ṇiye] hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā |

tasyāḥ pratisarāyāḥ paścimadiśi viśvapadmopari candramaṇḍalamadhye 
maṁkārabījapariṇārajāṃ mahāmantrānusāriṇīṃ bhāvayet śuklavarṇāṃ 
dvādaśabhujāṃ trinetrāṃ sphuratsūryamaṇḍalālīḍhāṃ ratnamukuṭinīṃ 
sarvālaṅkāraśobhitāṃ navayauvanopetāṃ hāranūpurakuṇḍalālaṅkārāṃ

ṣaṣṭhe śaraḥ tṛtīye tarjanīpāśaḥ caturthe dhanuḥ pañcame rtnacchaṭā [k: 
cchatrā] ṣaṣṭhe padmāṅkitakalaśaḥ [āll ḍ, dh, dh (k, g,

sarvasattvahitārthāya sarvasattvahitodayam |

pañcarakṣāvidhānaṃ ca likhyate svastyayanaṃ [k: tasyāyanammayā] mayā |

śucibhūmyāṃ śubhe ramye gomayenopalepite [k, g: lepayet] |
vitānavitate caiva nānāvastrapralambite ||

samantālliptagandhena candanena viśeṣataḥ |

śvetena raktacūrṇena [k: raja0] śāntikarma praśasyate |
padmasyāṣṭadalaṃ kuryāt karṇikākeśarānvitam ||

chatrapatākāsaṃyuktapallavena tu chāditam ||

digvidikṣu ca devānāṃ pūjayecca yathāvidhi ||

guḍabhaktaṃ śuklapuṣpaṃ pāyasaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
gandharvāṇāṃ baliṃ dattvā pūrvasthāne tu śtāpayet ||

p. 411)

tilakṛṣṇasurāpūrṇaṃn matsyamāṃsapalāṇḍakaiḥ |

īśānīṃ diśamārabhya yāvad vā pathagocare |
śuklaraktaṃ ca haritaṃ [k, g: pītaharitañca] sragdāma ca pralambitam 
[k, g: gdāmala0] ||

madhyaśvetaṃ sragdāma nānāpuṣpaviśeṣataḥ |
kṣīrarudhiraśarāvāṇāṃ sarjaragandhameva [k, g: lajjira0] ca ||

tathā ca -

snānaṃ kṛtvā śucirvastraṃ āsanaṃ ca śucirmatam ||

ācāryāṅgulimā kaścit pāṭhayet pariśuddhitaḥ |
ekavārādikārabhyaikaviṃśādi pravarttayet ||

ādau tāvanmantrī sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ śūnyāḥ sarvadharmā ityevamuccārya [k,

raśmisamūhaṃ bīje praviśantaṃ bhāvayet | tat sarvaṃ pariṇamya saṃgrāmatāriṇīṃ

alita0] vāmābhyāṃ tarjanīpāśakṛṣṇavajrāṅkitaśvetamūṣaladharāṃ

dadāmi sarvabuddhānāṃ triguhyālayasambhavam ||

mantramāvarttayennityaṃ satyavādī dayāparaḥ |

phaṭ phaṭ svāhā - hṛdayamantraḥ | oṃ sarvagrahanakṣatradhyāmīkaraṇi 

p. 417)

211.

nama āryoṣṇīṣavijayāyai |

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī mukhaśaucādikaṃ kṛtvā sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ 
caturbrahmavihārān bhāvayet | tadanantaraṃ svahṛdyakārapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ 
dhyātvā tadupari sitabhruṁkāraṃ dṛṣṭvā tadvinirgataraśmisamūhairjagadavabhāsya 
purataḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvān vicintya pūjāpāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | tataḥ punarapi svahṛdīndau paṁkārajaṃ 
viśvadalakamalaṃ dhyātvā tadupari candrabimbamadhyaśaṃsitabhruṁkāraṃ dṛṣṭvā 
tatpariṇatāṃ uṣṇīṣavijayāṃ caityaguhāntaḥsthāṃ sitavarṇāṃ trimukhāṃ aṣṭabhujāṃ 
sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitāṃ viśvadalakamalacandrasthāṃ [k: viśvaka0] 
baddhavajraparyaṅkāṃ prathamasitavadanāṃ dakṣiṇe pītamukhāṃ vāme nīlamukhāṃ 
daṃṣṭrāpuṭāvaṣṭabdhauṣṭhāṃ dakṣiṇacaturbhujeṣu viśvavajraraktāravindastha##-

p. 418)

dhanuḥtarjanīpāśa-abhayabhadraghaṭahastāṃ vairocanamukuṭinīṃ 
divyavasanaparidhānottarīyāṃ [k: adds nānālaṅkārabhūṣitāṃ after this] 
sitaprabhāmālinīṃ paśyet | 

tasyā dakṣiṇe lokeśvaro vāme padmadhārī dakṣiṇe cāmarahastaḥ vāme vajrapāṇiḥ 
kuvalayadalaśyāmaḥ vāme kuvalayasthavajradhārī dakṣiṇe cāmarahastaḥ | etau niṣaṇau 
cintanīyau | tataḥ dakṣiṇe cāmarahastaḥ | etau niṣaṇau cintanīyau | tataḥ 
pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottareṣu acalaṭakkirājanīladaṇḍamahābalāḥ [ḍh: ḍhakki0] 
| sarve nīlā dvibhujā ekamukhāḥ trinetrāḥ pratyālīḍhā vyāghracarmāmbarā ūrdhvakeśā 
aṣṭanāgābharaṇā viśvadalakamalasūryākrāntā vāme tarjanīpāśahastā dakṣiṇe 
khaḍga-aṅkuśavajradaṇḍahastā bhāvanīyāḥ | upari śuddhāvāsakāyikau 
devaputrau cintanīyau pūrṇakumbhamṛtāmṛtaṃ pravarṣamāṇau |

evaṃ saparivārāṃ bhagavatīṃ dhyātvā nyāsaṃ kuryāt | oṃ śirasi āḥ kaṇṭhe 
hūṁkāraṃ trāṁ lalāṭe hrīḥ nābhau aṃ aḥ pādayoḥ | tato mudrāṃ bandhayet 
sampuṭāñjaliṃ kṛtvā tarjanyau saṅkocya | jyeṣṭhāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ sādhukāraṃ 
dattvā oṃkāratrayasahitāṃ dhāraṇīmekavāramāvarttya paścāt vinā mudrayā āvarttayet | 
oṃ bhruṁ svāhā - hṛdayamantraḥ | oṃ amṛtāyurdade svāhā - 
upahṛdayamantraḥ | oṃ amite amitodbhave amitacakrānte amitagātre amitagāmini 
amitāyurdade gaganakīrtikari sarvakleśakṣayaṃkarīye svāhā - iti mālāmantraḥ |

|| āryoṣṇīṣavijayāsādhanaṃ samāptam ||

p. 419)

212.

praṇamya padmaṃ jagannāthaṃ dhyānāsaktajagatpriyam |
uṣṇīṣārādhanaṃ vakṣye svasmaraṇāya [k, g: sphāra0] sādhanam ||

kvacid deśe vihāre vā bhūdharādau manorame |
sukumārāsanāsīno yogī citte vicintayet ||

sattvānāṃ śaṅkaraṃ tatra sabhruṁkāraṃ sphuratprabham |
buddhān tasyāvabhāsena pūjayet tryadhvavarttinaḥ ||

sampūjya puratasteṣāṃ pāpaṃ kāyādikarmajam |
kṛtādibhedasañjātaṃ deśayedupadeśavit ||

anumodyaṃ śubhaṃ sarvaṃ tatprasūtaṃ viśodhayet |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ gacchet saprapañcaṃ [k, ḍh, a: sapta0] 
yathākramam ||

upadeśaṃ dṛḍhīkṛtya yāvadābodhisādhanam |
ālambya kṛpayā sattvān bodhicittaṃ vibhāvayet ||

nirmitaṃ ca nirālambaṃ niḥsvabhāvaṃ khadhātuvat |
tatrākārodbhavāmbhojasthite śītāṃśumaṇḍale ||

devīṃ tadbījasambhūtāṃ trimukhīṃ ca trilocanām |
navayauvanasampannāṃ śṛṅgārādirasāspadām ||

sarvabuddhaguṇākīrṇāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām |
prabhālīruddhasarvāśāṃ [k: lībuddha0, ḍh, a: lirvuddha0] 
śaradgaganasannibhām ||

jāmbūnadaprabhākārāṃ śāntasavyānanojjvalām |
pralayāmbhodharodbhāsibhīmatīvratarānanām ||

p. 420)

viśvahṛdayasaṃsaktakuliśaṃ [ḍh, a: sargī] savyapāṇinā |
dvitīyenāmbujāsīnaṃ buddhamanyena mārgaṇam ||

varadānaṃ caturthena vāmenāpi yathākramam |
tarjanīnihitaṃ pāśaṃ cāpaṃ cāpi tathābhayam ||

dadhānāṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃn ca dhyāyat caityaguhālayām |
sabījaṃ cintayet padmaṃ taddhṛdisthamanuttaram ||

tasya śuklaprabhājālacodanāśīlitaṃ nabhaḥ |
buddhameghairmahāvyūhairbodhisattvaiḥ samantataḥ ||

mahākṛpātmakāste'pi mantreṇa [k, g: santānāstrya0] 
tryadhvavarttinaḥ |
cintanīyāḥ prayatnena  sadā sekapradāyinaḥ ||

caityāntargatamoṁkāraṃ trāmākāraṃ prabhāsvaram |
hūṁ hrīḥ aṃ aḥ ityetad bījajātaṃ yathākramam ||

śikhālalāṭakaṇṭhasthaṃ hṛnnābhicaraṇasthitam |
candramaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ [k, g: madhye yā] dhyāyāt yogī 
maheśvaram ||

śaradinduprabhākārau sphuradgabhastimālinau |
kamalavajrasambhūtau savye cāmaradhāriṇau ||

padmavajradharau tasyāḥ pārśvayoḥ savyavāmayoḥ |
kekaraṃ purato'tyugraṃ ṭakkirājaṃ ca dakṣiṇe ||

nīladaṇḍaṃ tu pṛṣṭhasthaṃ vāme mahābalaṃ nyaset |
kṛpāṇāṅkuśasadvajradaṇḍasambhūtapāṇayaḥ ||

sadvidyudvajrasampātapralayāmbhodaniḥsvanāḥ |
grīṣmārkanayanālokaduṣṭaprāṇaharā [k, g: hṛṣṭu] amī ||

p. 421)

savye [k, g: sarve]  tarjanikāpāśairavasavyakarodyatāḥ |
jvālāmālākulā dhyeyā viśvapadmārkamaṇḍale ||

itthamaharniśaṃ mantrī bhāvayed yastu yogavit |
sa prāpnotyacirād bodhiṃ vajrayānapravarttanīm ||

|| ityuṣṇīṣavijayāsādhanam ||

213.

vasudhārāyai namaḥ |

pūrvavacchūnyatāparyantaṃ [g: nantaraṃ] vibhāvya jhaṭiti 
jambhalarūpamātmānaṃ dhyātvā svahṛdaye candramaṇḍalamadhye 
vaṁkārabījanirjātāṃ vasudhārāṃ bhagavatīṃ dhyāyāt [ḍh: dhyātvā] 
kanakavarṇāṃ sakalālaṅkāravatīṃ dviraṣṭavarṣākṛtiṃ dakṣiṇakareṇa varadāṃ 
[k has vāmakareṇa varadāṃ again] vāmakareṇa 
dhānyamañjarīdharāmakṣobhyadhāriṇīm | purato bhagavatīṃ śrīvasundharāṃ dakṣiṇato 
vasuśriyaṃ paścimataḥ śrīvasumukhīṃ vāmato vasumatiśriyam | 
etāścādyākṣarabījāḥ svanāyikāsamānarūpāścintanīyāḥ | evaṃ vibhāvya

sugandhikusumairabhyarcya sahasracatuṣṭayaṃ 

p. 422)

catuṣṭayaṃ japatāṃ ṣaṇmāsāt manorathaṃ paripūrayati [kh: pūra0] |

śrīrbhavati | 

|| iti vasudhārāsādhanam ||

|| vasudhārāsādhanam ||

nagnāṃ khaṇḍamaṇḍitamekhalāṃ śatārddhasārdranaraśiromālāpralambitāṃ 
[k, g; sārddha0] vāmabhuje kapālaṃ duṣṭamārādyasṛgdharaṃ dakṣiṇe 
- 

tarjayantīṃ diśaḥ sarvaduṣṭatarjanavajrikām [kh: sarvā] |
kalpavahnimahātejāṃ sravantīṃ rudhirapriyām ||

ūrdhvapādasthitāṃ bhāvayet | padmasya pūrvādipatre - 

ḍākinīṃ tu tathā lāmāṃ khaṇḍarohāṃ tu rūpiṇīm |
vidikpatre tathā bhāvyāḥ karoṭāścatvāraḥ śobhanāḥ ||

mantraṃ cātra - oṃ sarvabuddhaḍākinīye vajravarṇanīye hūṁ hūṁ [ḍh: adds a 
third hūṁ] phaṭ phaṭ [kh, g: phaṭ only] svāhā |

|| śrī [kh: oḍyāna0] 
oḍiyānavajrapīṭhavinirgataūrdhvapādavajravārāhīsādhanaṃ [kh: omits 
ūrddhvapāda0] samāpatam ||

p. 440)

226.

prathamaṃ tāvad yogī śmaśānādau manorame sthāne svahṛdīndau 
oṃkārakiraṇairgurubuddhabodhisattvānānīya purato'valambya pūjāpāpadeśanādikaṃ 
kṛtvā khasamaṃ traidhātukaṃ vicintya hūṁvajrīkṛtabhūmau śmaśānāṣṭakamadhye 
dharmodayāntargatapadmavaraṭake candrastharavisomasampuṭasthavaṁkāravajraṃ 
tadvījasamudbhavāṃ bhagavatīṃ vajravārāhīṃ pralayānalasannibhāṃ ekavaktrāṃ 
dvibhujāṃ dakṣiṇe - 

tarjayantīṃ diśaḥ sarvaduṣṭatarjanavajrikām |

vāme kapālaṃ [k, ḍh, g: kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharāṃ] 
viśvapatākāvirājitabāhudaṇḍāsaktakhaṭvāṅgadharāṃ bhairavakālarātryākrāntāṃ 
pratyālīḍhena tāṇḍavāṃ digvāsāṃ muktakeśāṃ khaṇḍamaṇḍitamekhalāṃ 
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ trinetrāṃ vikṛtānanāṃ viśvavajradharāṃ mūrdhni 
vajramālākapālaśobhitāṃ [k, ḍh; vajraka, kh: vakra0] 
muṇḍasragdāmadehobhayaśobhāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitāṅgīṃ 
sarvabuddhābhiṣekajāṃ vairocanakulodbhavāṃ sarvasiddhipradāyikāṃ 
sphuratsaṃhāravigrahām - 

pūjāstutyamṛtāsvādaṃ [k: nṛtā0] kṛtvā yogī samāhitaḥ |
sādaraṃ bhāvayan nityaṃ laghu buddhatvamāpnute [k, ḍh, g, kh: 
mavāpyate] ||

bhāvanāṃ kṛtvā nyāsaṃ kuryāt baliṃ ca dadyāt yogavit | oṃ vaṁ vajravārāhī nābhau 
hāṁ [k, g: haṁ] yāṁ yāminī hṛdi hrīṁ moṁ

p. 441)

mohanī vaktre hreṁ [k, ḍh: hūṁ] hrīṁ sañcālanī śirasi hūṁ hūṁ 
santrāsanī śikhāyām phaṭ phaṭ caṇḍikā [ṭhe ḍ, dh, kh: ends here] 
sarvāṅgeṣvastram - 

nābhau hṛdi tathā vaktre śiraḥ śikhāstrameva ca |

kṛtvāgragraṃthyā [ṭhe metre in this śloka is faulty] khalu madhyasūcī 
aṅguṣṭhavajro dṛḍhaṃ saṃprapīḍya |
saṃsthāpyatāmadho lalāṭadeśe āvarttyāvarttena ca vibhrāmayet ||

ākrāntapādorddhvadṛṣṭistu mūrdhnā pheṃkāranādataḥ daśadiglokadhātusthā 
vīrayoginīḥ ākarṣayet - oṃ yogaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ yogaśuddho'haṃ oṃ 
acaṇḍi [k, g: ārali0] hoḥ jaḥ hūṁ vaṁ hoḥ vajraḍākinyaḥ samayastvaṃ 
dṛśyahoḥ | vajrāñjalyā ūrdhvavikacabaliṃ dadyānniśārddhake - oṃ kha kha 
khāhi khāhi sarvayakṣarākṣasabhūtapretapiśāconmādāpasmāraḍākaḍākinyādaya imaṃ 
baliṃ gṛhṇantu samayaṃ rakṣantu samayasiddhiṃ [k, g: mama] 
prayacchantu yathaivaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ bhuñjatha pibatha mātikramatha mama [k, g; 
omit] sarvākārasatsukhaviśuddhaye sahāyakā bhavantu hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ [g: 
omits] svāhā - balimantraḥ | oṃ vajravairocanīye hūṁ [g: omits hūṁ 
phaṭ] phaṭ svāhā - hṛdayamantraṃ daśākṣaram | oṃ sarvabuddhaḍākinīye 
vajravarṇanīye hūṁ hhūṁ phaṭ phaṭ [g; omits] svāhā | 
upahṛdayamekaviṃśatyakṣaram | oṃ sarvavajrakāmini sarvabhakṣyaṃ śodhaya 
guhyavajriṇi hūṁ svāhā - sarvabhakṣyaśodhanamantraḥ | 

p. 442)

athavā upadeśaḥ - svacittaṃ sthirīkarttukāmaḥ svanābhikamalopari 
somapuṭāntasthasarṣapasūkṣmavaṁkāravinirgatamṛṇālatantvākāraraśmiṃ dhyāyāt 
traidhātukaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ mahāsukhākāraṃ paśyediti |

|| saṃkṣiptavajravārāhīsādhanaṃ samāptam ||

227.

namo vajravārāhyai |

nabhonibhaṃ jaganmatvā sambodhiṃ ca tathā manaḥ |
sthāpayāmi jagad bodhau vajravārāhikātmakaḥ ||

mano'nukūle śmaśānādau svahṛdi candre oṃkārakiraṇairnabhasi 
gurubuddhabodhisattvān dṛṣṭvā pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | tato nirālambaśūnyatāṃ 
bhāvayet | hūṁvajrībhūtabhūmyādau [k, g: bhṛkuṭa, ḍh: bhṛta0] 
śmaśānāṣṭakamadhye dharmadhātumayadharmodayāntargatapadmavaraṭake 
candrastharavisomasampuṭasthavaṁkāravajrasambhavāṃ bhagavatīṃ bhāvayet 
vajravārāhīṃ pralayānalasannibhāṃ dvibhujaikavaktrāṃ dakṣiṇe tarjayantīṃ 
duṣṭatarjanavajrikāṃ vāme kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharāṃ bhairavakālarātryākrāntāṃ 
pratyālīḍhena satāṇḍavāṃ digambarāṃ muktakeśāṃ khaṇḍamaṇḍitamekhalāṃ 
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ trinetrāṃ viśvavajradharāṃ vajramālākapālamūrdhvajāṃ 
muṇḍasragdāmadehaśobhāṃ sarvālaṅkāraśobhitāṃ [ḍh: bhūṣitāṃ] 
sarvabuddhābhiṣekāṃ vairocanakulodbhavāṃ sarvasiddhipradāyikāṃ 
sphuratsaṃhāravigrahām |

p. 443)

pūjāstutyamṛtāsvādaṃ [k, g: svādanaṃ] kṛtvā [ḍh: adds yogo 
after this] yogaparāyaṇaḥ |
sādaraṃ bhāvayan nityaṃ laghu buddhatvamāpnuyāt ||

bhāvanākhinno mantraṃ japet - oṃ vajravairocanīye svāhā | atropadeśaḥ 
svanābhikamalopari somasampuṭāntargatamṛṇālatantvākāraraśmiṃ dhyāyāt | tataḥ 
traidhātukaṃ prabhāsvaratayā tat cittaṃ sukhaśāntamādyamanutpannaṃ paśyediti |

|| saṃkṣiptavajravārāhīsādhanaṃ samāptam ||

228.

namo nairātmyāyai |

ḍombīherukapādairdevīcakrasya bhāvanā kathitā |
sañcintya sakalaṃ tattvaṃ he [k, g: vajrayo] vajrāt yoginītantrāt ||

nabhasi bhaṭṭārakaṃ dṛṣṭvā gurukuliśeśvaraṃ prabhum |
pūjayet vandayet dhīmānakṣobhyapratirūpakam ||

svabījaṃ hṛdaye dhyātvā pūjāmeghaṃ prakalpayet |
aṣṭābhirmātṛbhirnityaṃ pañcāmṛtaprayogataḥ ||

gauryādyāḥ [ṭhis śloka is omitted in k] pañca yoginyaḥ 
pañcāmṛtadharā matāḥ [g: mṛtāḥ] |
śabarī caṇḍālī [ḍh: linī] ḍombī rasārdrāliṅganairyutāḥ [k: 
rasamudrasarvā, g: rasadramarvā (?), ḍh: rasaḍamarva (##?

aghaṃ ca deśayennityaṃ puṇyaṃ caivānumodayet |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ yāyād bodhau cittaṃ bibhartti ca ||

p. 444)

prathamaṃ dhyāyānmaitrīṃ tadanu karuṇāṃ vibhāvayet |
punarapi muditāṃ dhyāyādupekṣāṃ sarvaśeṣataḥ ||

tadanu jinamantraṃ paṭhet - oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | svaparaṃ 
vibhāvya śūnyaṃ praṇidhānamanusmared yogī | 

repheṇa sūryaṃ purato vibhāvya tasmin ravau hūṁbhavaviśvavajram |
tenaiva vajreṇa vibhāvayecca prākāratatpañjarabandhanaṃ ca ||

khadhātau [k, g: svadhā0] cintayed dhīmān prajñāmekārarūpiṇīm 
[k, g: jñāname0] |
tasmin vibhāvayet pṛthvīṃ caturasrāṃ harītikām ||

pṛthvīmaṇḍalamadhyasthāṃ abmaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvayet |
apsu tejaḥ punardhyāyādagnau ca marunmaṇḍalam ||

marunmaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ ātmānaṃ cintayet tataḥ |
miśrībhūya bhaveccakramekākāreṇa [k: cakramava, ḍh: cakramevaṃ] 
cātmanā ||

catuṣkoṇaṃ caturdvāraṃ aṣṭastambhopaśobhitam |
hārārddhahārasaṃyuktaṃ vajrasūtrairalaṅkṛtam ||

tasmiṃścakre gataprāṇān pañcadaśaṃ vibhāvayet |
yoginīkakubhoddeśāt āsanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ matam ||

āsanopari candraṃ [candraṃ ca omitted in k, g] ca candrasyopari 
bhāskaram |
anayormadhyagataṃ bījaṃ mūlasvarādikaṃ nyaset ||

ādarśasamatājñānau candrasūryau prakīrttitau |
aṅkārasambhavā kartriḥ pratyavekṣaṇamucyate ||

p. 445)

eṣāmaikyamanuṣṭhānaṃ niṣpattiḥ śuddhadharmatā |
pañcajñānātmakaṃ dehaṃ yoginīnāṃ prakīrtitam ||

candrārkābhyāṃ ca karttryā śabalībhūtaṃ yathā jalam |
miśrībhūya punaḥ paścāt jñeyā nairātmyayoginī ||

kṛṣṇavarṇā mahāghorā pañcamudrāvibhūṣitā |
savye kartrirmahābhīmā'vasavye padmabhājanam |
avasavye vajrakhaṭvāṅgaṃ vyāghracarmāvṛtā [vṛtā kaṭiḥ in k, 
ḍh, g] kaṭau ||

a ā i ī u ca kathitāni tu nairātmyādīnām ū ṛ ṝ ḷ iti ca proktāni pukkasyādīnām 
[k, g; prakka0] ḹ e ai o bījānīti gauryādīnām au aṃ bīje punarapi kathite 
adhorddhvavāsinyāḥ | cittaśāśvataratneśāmitābhāmoghasiddhibhiḥ tān mudrayet 
[k: omits mudrayet pukkasyādīn] nairātmyādīn prajñāpuṭe vyavasthitān |

cittaśāśvataratneśa-amitāyustathāgataiḥ |
vidiksthān pukkasyādīn mudrayedanulomataḥ ||

dveṣamohapiśunaistu rāgavajreṇa mudraṇam |
yoginīnāṃ catasṝṇāṃ dvārasthānāṃ sadaiva tu ||

kāyavāṅmudraṇaṃ kuryāt adhorddhveṣu samāhitaḥ |
mudryate [k: muḍya0] lakṣaṇeneti [ḍh: teneti] mudraṇaṃ tena 
bhaṇyate ||

indre vajrā yame gaurī vāruṇyāṃ vāriyoginī |
kaubere vajraḍākī [k, g: kinī] ca madhye nairātmyayoginī ||

aiśāne pukkasī khyātā pāvake śabarī tathā |
caṇḍālī rākṣasāśāyāṃ vāyavye ḍombinī [k, g: ḍākinī] tathā ||

p. 446)

pūrvadvāre punargaurī dakṣiṇe caurikā tathā |
vetālī paścime dvāre uttare ghasmarī tathā ||

adhare bhūcarī khyātā ūrdhve ca khecarī smṛtā |
paramānandadāḥ sarvā yathā nairātmyayoginī ||

svabījaṃ hṛdaye dhyātvā raśmīn niścārayet tataḥ |
ākṛṣya tena sambuddhān pūjayedaṣṭamātṛbhiḥ ||

paścādanunāthanāṃ [k, g: nāsi devī] vidadhīta bhagavatsu mahātmasu |
bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatā abhiṣekapadaṃ māṃ dadantu ||

paścādamṛtabhṛtaiḥ kumbhairabhiṣicyate budhaḥ |
secayan sarvasambuddhaiḥ citteśamukuṭo bhavet ||

prathamaṃ bhāvayet kṛṣṇāṃ dvitīye raktāṃ vibhāvayet |
tṛtīye bhāvayet pītāṃ caturthe haritāṃ tathā ||

pañcame nīlavarṇābhāṃ ṣaṣṭhe suśukladehikām |
ṣaḍaṅgāṃ bhāvayed yogī paścād varṇavivarjitām ||

rūpaskandhe bhaved vajrā gaurī vedanāyāṃ smṛtā |
saṃjñāyāṃ vāriyoginī saṃskāre vajraḍākinī ||

vijñānaskandharūpeṇa sthitā nairātmyayoginī |
maṇḍale sā sthitā bhīmā bhayasyāpi bhayaṅkarī ||

pṛthivī pukkasī khyātā abdhātuḥ śabarī matā |
tejaścaṇḍālinī jñeyā vāyurḍombī prakīrtitā ||

rūpe gaurī sadā khyātā śabde caurī [k: raurī] prakīrtitā |
vetālī gandhaviṣaye rase ca ghasmarī tathā || 

p. 447)

sparśe ca bhūcarī khyātā khecarī dharmadhātutaḥ |
sadā hyasāraviśuddhyā vai sidhyanti tattvayoginaḥ [k, g: ginīḥ] ||

cakṣuṣormohavajrī ca karṇayordveṣavajrikā |
ghrāṇe [k, g: omit this line] mātsaryakī khyātā vaktre ca rāgavajrikā |
sparśe īrṣyāvajrī ca mano nairātmyayoginī ||

kavacamebhirmahāśuddhyā indriyāṇāṃ viśuddhaye |
navamāryaṃ dhṛtaṃ muṇḍaṃ kartrikā devacchedanī ||

kapālakleśamuṇḍena skandhasyopari cāśanam [k, g: sādhanaṃ] |
gurvācāryeṣṭadevasya namanārthaṃ cakrikā dhṛtā ||

durbhāṣasyāśravaṇāya gurorvajradharasya ca |
kuṇḍalaṃ śravaṇayordhāryaṃ mantraṃ japtuṃ ca kaṇṭhikā ||

rucakaḥ prāṇivadhaṃ [k, g: bandhaṃ] tyaktuṃ mudrāṃ bhajituṃ ca 
[k, g: bhañjituñca] mekhalām |
pañcabuddhasya sūtreṇa śarīraṃ mudritaṃ sadā ||

mantraḥ | oṃ a ā i ī u ū ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ e ai o au aṃ aḥ svāhā | mātrāviśuddhiṃ 
kathayāmi - 

akṣobhyaścakrirūpeṇāmitābhaḥ kuṇḍalātmakaḥ |
ratneśaḥ kaṇṭhamālāyāṃ haste vairocanaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

mekhalāyāṃ sthito'moghaḥ pañcaite śuddhamātrikāḥ |
doṣaparihāṇito dhāryā yoginībhiḥ sadā dhṛtāḥ ||

adhyātmarūpaṃ kathayāmi - 

strīrūpeṇa vaśaṃ [k: omits vaśaṃ rūpeṇa] loke krodharūpeṇa trāsanam |
ālikālisamāpattyā bāhyadvandvaṃ niṣevitam ||

p. 448)

purakṣobhamantraḥ - akacaṭatapayasa svāhā | sārvabhautikabalimantraḥ - oṃ 
akāro mukhaṃ sarvadharmāṇāmādyamanutpannatvāt oṃ āḥ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā |

nairātmyayoginyāścaiva saṃvaraṃ kathayāmi te |
mahāmudrā sthitā nābhau mahāsukhakarā [ḍ, dh, dh: kālikā or 
kārikā] śubhā |
tīkṣṇatvādagnirūpeṇa ruṣitā sā tu mātṛbhiḥ ||

ādisvarasvabhāvā sā dhīti buddhaiḥ prakalpitā |
vyāpya tiṣṭhati traidhātuṃ [k, g: tukaṃ] na jñātā sā pṛthagjanaiḥ ||

nairātmyakamadhyasthaṃ [k, g: nānā0] ca anāhatamadhomukham 
[k: madhye sukhaṃ] |
sravatyanirvartakayogena [k, g: pravartta0] bodhicittaṃ sadaiva tu ||

tasmāt kāye'tra ye (nāḍyo) lalanārasanāvadhūtayaḥ |
kāyavākcittarūpeṇāvatīrṇāḥ kartrimudrikāḥ [ḍh: karṇa0] ||

lalanā prajñāsvabhāvena rasanopāyena saṃsthitā |
avadhūtī madhyadeśe tu mahāsukhādhārarūpiṇī ||

akṣobhyāvahā lalanā rasanā raktapravāhinī |
avadhūtyamitanāthasya ādhārabhāvinī sadā ||

karmamudrāsukhāvatyāṃ trayo nāḍyaḥ svakālataḥ [k, g: svabhāvataḥ] 
|
sravanti kāyavākcittaṃ vajraratnābjajanminam ||

mūtrakālastu vajrasya vamantaṃ ratnadhāriṇam |
abhisaṃbuddharūpeṇāmitanāthasya kathyate ||

sadaivotpadyate jñānamuktaṃ vāvasare'pi vā |
sahajaṃ paramaṃ saukhyaṃ pitrorapi sukhāvaham ||

p. 449)

yathā niśāyāṃ hi bhunakti kaścit veśmāntare dīpitadīpakaḥ san |
paryeṣṭikarttustanubhistamovat mohāndhakāreṇa sukhaṃ tathā syāt ||

jñātvaivaṃ bhavasāgaraṃ viṣajalaṃ rāgādinakrākulaṃ 
gambhīrālaharīlatāvighaṭitaṃ saukhyaikatīraṃ mahat |
kasmāt bodhidhiyo janāḥ kṣaṇamapi svārthaikaniṣṭhodyatāḥ [k: 
ṣṭhāyutāḥ, ḍh: ṣṭhoyutāḥ] pāraṃ yānti na mohapāśavasanaṃ tyaktvā 
prayāntyadbhutam [k, g; samāsā] ||

utpattikramabhāvanāṃ prathamataḥ kuryāt sudhīḥ śāśvataṃ 
mudrāmantraviśuddhimātrakavacaṃ sarvānna vai vismaret |
paścādutkarṣamanvitaḥ pratimuhustattvaikacetāḥ 
sudhīrutpannakramabhāvanābhavabhidaṃ dhyāyāt samastaṃ sadā ||

vyāghrāsīnaścared yo vividhaphaṇivarairbhūṣito yaḥ kṣamāvān bāḍhaṃ [k: 
bhrāṭā] sambhogayute vividhamadhuyute kaṅkagehākhyanāmni |
tenedaṃ sādhanendraṃ [k, ḍh: daṃ] parahitarucinā 
guhyaprajñāṅgasaṅgaṃ [k, g: nāṅguṣṭha] lokārthodbhāsitaṃ yat 
kṣamata jinasutā bhāṣitaṃ yanmayedam ||

|| amṛtaprabhā nāma sādhanopāyikā samāptā ||

229.

kulejanmānunmādī [k, g: omit the first two ślokas] samayī 
hevajradeśakaḥ |
kṛpāvān gurubhaktaśca bhaveyaṃ janmajanmani ||

vajraghaṇṭāraṇatpāṇirgambhīradharmapāṭhakaḥ |
yoṣicchukrasamāhārī bhaveyaṃ janmajanmani ||

mohavajrī kathaṃ kṛṣṇā mohavajrasvabhāvataḥ |
rūpaskandho bhavenmoho rūpaścitteśalakṣitam ||

anurāgaikahetutvādatītairvāruṇaṃ manaḥ |
anurāgimanaścihnaṃ rāgavajrābhalakṣaṇam ||

rāgavajrā kathaṃ śyāmā rāgo rakto yato mataḥ |
caturvarṇairiyaṃ yasmānniṣpannā śyāmavarṇataḥ ||

ṣaṭtathāgatarūpo'yamamoghasambhavo mataḥ |
asyāḥ ṣaḍrasarūpiṇyāṃ [k, g: sadrasa0] śyāmatvenopalakṣaṇam ||

īrṣyā mohasamudbhūtā moho vairocano mataḥ |
prajñopāyādataścerṣyā cittavajrābhalakṣaṇā ||

cittameva mano yasmāt jñānasattvāḥ kathaṃ sitāḥ |
manorūpādṛtaṃ na syāt teneyaṃ rūpamudritā ||

ṣaḍ vijñaptiḥ sadā ṣoḍhā yoginīmūrtivarjitāḥ |
pūrvapakṣaparīhārasphuṭīkṛtena yat śubham ||

mayāptaṃ tena te lokā mantramārge suniścayam |
buddhvā buddhatvamabhrāntyā prāpnuvantu kṣaṇādamī ||

ghrāṇendriye cīkāraṃ [k, g: ri0] pītaṃ jihvendriye āḥkāraṃ sitaṃ 
uṣṇīṣasthāne rephaṃ haritaṃ manasi khakāraṃ sitaṃ cintayet iti ṣaḍindriye 
ṣaḍakṣaranyāsaḥ kavacam | oṃ āḥ hūṁ iti kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaṃ yathākramaṃ 
śiraḥkaṇṭhahṛdayeṣu | stanāntare [k, g: stadana] sūrye svadevatābījaṃ 
tajjñānasvabhāvaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatkiraṇāṅkuśairjñānamaṇḍalamākṛṣya 
hūṁkāreṇa vighnānutsārya arghyaṃ dattvā jaḥ hūṁ vaṁ hoḥ ityākṛṣya praveśya 
baddhā 

p. 462)

vaśīkṛtya tena sahaikalolībhūtaṃ samayamaṇḍalaṃ paśyet | tato 
jñānabījaraśmicoditatathāgatapreṣitāḥ pañcayoginya āgatya pañcajñānāmbupūrṇai 
ratnakalaśairbhagavantaṃ yoginyaścābhiṣiñceyuḥ | abhiṣicyamānānāṃ śirasi 
svakuleśa utpadyate bhagavato'kṣobhyaḥ buddhaḍākinyādīnāṃ yathākramaṃ 
śāśvatākṣobhyaratneśavāgīśāmoghasiddhayaḥ | tato 
jñānabījaraśmimukhasphāritābhiḥ [k, g: sukha] 
pañcopacārapañcakāmaguṇapūjābhirbhagavantaṃ bhagavatīṃ ca pūjayet | tato 
bhagavantaṃ devīnāṃ mukhaiḥ stūyāt - 

daṃṣṭrotkaṭamahābhīmamuṇḍasragdāmabhūṣitam |
bhakṣamāṇaṃ mahāmāṃsaṃ śrīherukaṃ namāmyaham ||

iti | tato digdevyaḥ sphuraṇayogena buddhaḍākinyāmantarbhaveyuḥ | tato'syāḥ śirasi 
hṛdi nābhau guhye ūrvośca [k: bhruvo] yathākramaṃ oṃ hūṁ svā 
[k, g: svāhā] ā hā ityetān vibhāvya tairanyonyāvadhi sphuradbhirasyāḥ 
śarīramabhivyāpya tayā hrīḥkārajaraktakamalayā akārajamuktākarṇikayā tadvat 
hūṁkārajakuliśaoṃkārajamaṇiḥ prārabheta ratiṃ anurāgaṃ yāmītyanena mantreṇa | tato 
maṇidvayaraśmicoditāstathāgatā āgatya mukhe praviśya vilīya kamaladale pateyuḥ | tat 
pañcajñānāmṛtaṃ jihvāgralalitena gṛhyamāṇaṃ cintayediti |

|| śrīmaṇḍalarājāgrīnāma dvitīyaḥ samādhiḥ ||

tato bhagavaccoditā [k, g: nno] buddhaḍākinyādayaḥ pañcasu [k, g: 
skakīyañca] pañcabhagavatkāyameghasphuraṇaiḥ [k, g: sphuraṇādau] 
sattvānāmarthaṃ yathākramaṃ ādarśajñānaṃ 

p. 463)

suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānaṃ samatājñānaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānaṃ

saṃhareyuriti [k: lūna] |

|| karmarājāgrīnāma tṛtīyaḥ samādhiḥ ||

yadi punaryogī cittaṃ sthirīkartumicchet sarvasamādhīnāmagraniṣpattaye [k, g: 
māśu] tadā samāsataḥ samādhitrayaṃ vibhāvya nābhikamalakarṇikāyāṃ 
ravisomasampuṭaprānteṣu niḥsandhiṃ dhyātvā tanmadhye 
sarṣapasūkṣmasitasvacchajñānaṃ bindurūpaṃ mahāsukhamayaṃ vīraṃ vicintya 
tadraśmirekhāṃ mṛṇālatantunibhāṃ candramadhyena nirgatya sampuṭaṃ tiryak 
tribhirveṣṭaiḥ saṃyamya sthitāṃ dṛṣṭvā candropari sthiteṣu triṣu veṣṭārddheṣu 
grathitān pratyekaṃ ṣoḍaśa varṇān paśyet | uktaṃ hi sambarottare - 

kakārādidakārāntaṃ ṅañvarjaṃ niveśayet |
dhakārādihakārāntaṃ ālidvayaṃ samālikhet |
madhyasthena tvakārālirnyastavyaḥ śaśimaṇḍale ||

iti | ihāpyuktaṃ divyamakṣarapaṅktibhiriti | evaṃ nābhimadhye 
kamalakarṇikāravisomasaṃpuṭagarbhe vīraṃ bhāvayan bhāvanāparipākamadhimucya 
tasmādeva dvitīyasūkṣmaraśmirekhājvalitaikajvālāvadavasthitāṃ paśyet | tasyāḥ 
prabhāvena sravadamṛtapūraplāvito vīraḥ sā ca raśmirekhā sitībhavet | tato vīre 
niyamitacittastaṃ [k, g: cittavattāṃ] paśyet yāvat cetasaḥ sthairyalābhaṃ 
nimittairupalakṣayet | upalakṣite sthairye tasyā raśmirekhāyāḥ sphuṭamālokamanantaṃ 
viśvamavabhāsayantaṃ 

p. 464)

vicintya sarvaṃ karatalāmalakavat paśyet sarvasamādhīṃ ścāśu niṣpādayet |

iti binduyogaḥ |

japtavidyasya siddhiriti japa ucyate | tathaiva [k: vāpade] ṣaḍdevatācakraṃ yogī

raśmirekhāmucchvāsavāyunā sahotthāpya tayāvabhāsyajagad buddhamayaṃ kuryāt |

namo herukāya |

242.

ākāśalakṣaṇaṃ sarvamākāśaṃ cāpyalakṣaṇam |
māyopamaṃ ca sarvaṃ [k, g: sarvatra] vai traidhātukamaśeṣataḥ ||

dṛśyate spṛśyate caiva yathā māyā hi sarvataḥ |
na copalabhyate caiva sarvasya jagataḥ sthitiḥ ||

ityarthaṃ manasādhimucya ekānekaviyogāt svaśarīraṃ ca sakalaṃ ca nīlādivastujātaṃ 
śūnyamākalayya svacittaṃ manovijñānākhyamanantaramakāraṃ sūkṣmaṃ 
nistaraṅgamapratighaṃ [k, g: raṃśu ; k: ghnaṃ] nirupamavarṇasaṃsthānaṃ 
bhrāntimadapyupakārāyayatrābhyantaranivṛttaye [k, g: nici0] ityāropya 
lakṣyasthirīkaraṇārthaṃ khadyotākāramadhimucya tacca śūnyatākaruṇāgarbhaṃ 
sakalakalāparipūrṇacandramaṇḍalākāraṃ vicintayet | etasmin candramaṇḍale 
upacitapuṇyasambhāralakṣaṇe upacitajñānasambhāralakṣaṇaṃ hrīḥkāraṃ nīlavarṇaṃ 
paśyet [g: nyaset] tatpariṇataṃ ca nīlajvālāvajramādarśajñānasvabhāvaṃ 
cintayet candravajrākārapariṇataṃ jinanivahamanyūdbhavaśrīkaruṇācalakrodhaṃ 
herukākhyamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet adhomukhasya śavasyopari viśvapadmaṃ tasyopari 
sūryamaṇḍalaṃ tanmadhye samupaviṣṭaṃ ekasyorddhvabhujadvayaṃ iti vacanāt 
arddhaparyaṅkinaṃ bhasmoddhūlitaraktaprabhāmālinaṃ piṅgalorddhvakeśaṃ 
akṣobhyādyalaṅkṛtakapālamālāmukuṭinaṃ dakṣiṇakareṇa 
tripatākāgṛhītanīlakarālavajraṃ [g: kapāla] 
vāmaskandhāvasaktavajrakhaṭvāṅgaṃ raktaparipūrṇakapālavāmakaraṃ 
sārdranaramastakamālākṛtasragdāmadaṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ 

p. 471)

caladvarttulākāraraktākṣaṃ savibhramabhṛkuṭinaṃ paramārthato vācyaṃ 
vicāraśūnyatvāt saṃvṛtyā svapnasaudāminīgandharvanagaracchāyāmāyopamaṃ 
caturmudrāmudritam sveṣṭadevatāvigrahaḥ sarvasiddhiprado bhavati - mahāmudrā 
samayamudrā dharmamudrā karmamudrā | svahṛdi candrama.ḍalasyopari vajraṃ vibhāvya 
pañcasūcikaṃ varṇabhujasaṃsthānopetaṃ cihnasahitaṃ śrīherukamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet 
iti mahāmudrā | tathaiva svahṛdi candravajraṃ vibhāvya vajrāñjaliṃ mūrdhni vikacāṃ

iti stutiḥ |

tathaiva sūrye nīlahūṁkārjajaṃ paramāśvaṃ raktaṃ caturmukhaṃ aṣṭabhujaṃ 
catuścaraṇaṃ prathamamukhaṃ krodhaśṛṅgāraṃ trilocanaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ raudraṃ 
vāmaṃ brahmamukhaṃ mūrdhni lalitoddhūlitoṣṭhaṃ [kh: 
lalitorddhvalalitoṣṭhaṃ] haritāśvamukhaṃ ekena dakṣiṇatripatākādharakereṇa 
viśvavajrasahitenottiṣṭhābhinayaṃ kurvantaṃ ekena [kh: etena] 
vāmakheṭakahastena [kh: khaḍga] viśvapadmaṃ dhārayantaṃ 
punardakṣiṇatripatākākareṇottiṣṭhābhinayaṃ kurvantaṃ [k, g: add 
punarvāmakareṇottiṣṭhābhinayaṃ kurvantaṃ after this] punarvāmakareṇa śaktiṃ 
dhārayantaṃ punardakṣiṇakarābhyāṃ khaḍgaṃ [k, g: khaṭvāṅga] bāṇaṃ 
ca avaśiṣṭavāmakarābhyāṃ daṇḍaṃ cāpaṃ ca dhārayantaṃ pratyālīḍhena 
dakṣiṇapādaikenendrāṇīṃ śriyaṃ cākramya sthitaṃ 

p. 511)

dvitīyadakṣiṇacaraṇena ratiṃ prītiṃ ca vāmaprathamapādaikenendraṃ madhukaraṃ ca 
vāmadvitīyapādena jayakaraṃ vasantaṃ ca ityātmānaṃ dhyāyāt iti | mudrā - 
viśvavajratripatākottiṣṭhā paramāśvamudrā | mantraḥ - oṃ śumbha niśumbha 
hūṁ |

|| iti paramāśvasādhanam ||

caturmukhaṃ aṣṭabhujaṃ prathamamukhaṃ krodhaśṛṅgāraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ raudraṃ

krodharājamūlamudrāṃ baddhvā hūṁ vajra [kh: vajrasphaṭ] phaṭ | 
dṛśyasamayaṃ [k, g: dṛśaṃ, ḍh: dṛśā] dadyāt | tato bhagavataḥ 
pūjāṃ kṛtvā krodhāveśamudrāṃ baddhvā vajrakrodhaṃ svaśarīre praveśayet 
[k, g: omit] | oṃ praviśya vajrakrodha hūṁ hūṁ hūṁ āḥ 
jvālāmālākulabhīṣaṇavajra āḥ | svadehe [k, g: dehama] antarbhāvya 
kavacādikaṃ kṛtvā sarvametat pūraṇārthaṃ sampuṭāñjaliṃ baddhvā 
pūraṇamantramuccārayet | oṃ siddhivajra āpūraya āpūraya hūṁ phaṭ | anena sarva 
ūnātiriktāḥ paripūrṇā bhavanti | tato vajrakrodho'haṃ ityahaṅkāramutpādya [k, 
g: dhāhaṅkā] sarvasiddhimākarṣayet | anena mudrāmantreṇa anyonyamuṣṭiṃ 
kṛtvā kaniṣṭhādvayaṃ veṣṭayet tarjanīṃ prasārya kuṇḍalīṃ kṛtvā kiñciccālayet ##-
candramaṇḍalasthān ūrdhvaśikhān akṣarān [k, ḍh, g: netānya] vicintya 
nīlālīmiva paṅktitaḥ oṃ vajra [k, ḍh, g: hūṁ] phaṭ | tataḥ 
saromakūpebhyaḥ krodhavigrahaṃ sphuratsaṃhārakārakaṃ vicintayet | bhāvanākhinno 
japamārabheta tanmānaso varṇagataikacitto japet | khinne krodhavigrahaṃ 
svahṛdaye'ntarbhāvya

p. 524)

sampuṭāñjaliṃ baddhvā [k, ḍh, g: kṛtvā] sarvasiddhiṃ prārthayet 
siddhivajra āpūraya sarvasattvānāmāśāṃ sarvasiddhiṃ me prayaccha hūṁ jaḥ [ḍh: 
adds pūrvoktaṃ pūjayet after this] | tataḥ padmamudrā dvābhyāṃ aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ 
cālayet sarvadevatāvisarjanamudrā | mantraḥ oṃ sara sara visara visara gaccha [g: omits 
gaccha gaccha] gaccha sarvadevatān śrīvajradharaḥ samājñāpayati svāhā | tataḥ 
svaśarīraṃ kavacayitvā krodhāhaṅkāramānaso vihared yathāsukhaṃ ityanena krameṇa | 
mantralakṣaṃ japet kṣipraṃ sidhyati | atha mantrī abhiṣikto'nujñātaḥ kṛtapūrvasevo 
vajradharaṃ sādhayitukāmo māsamekaṃ trisandhyaṃ sahasramekaṃ japet | tataḥ 
pūrṇamāsyāṃ yathāvibhavataḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā susamāhitaḥ krodhamudrāṃ baddhvā 
sakalāṃ rātriṃ japet | tataḥ prabhāte bhūḥ kampate mudrā jvalati jvalitamātreṇa 
vajradharasadṛśo bhavati ajarāmaradivyarūpī bhavatītyāha bhagavān vajradharaḥ |

|| saṃkṣiptasampūrṇavispaṣṭakramasaṅgatabhūtaḍāmarasādhanopāyikā [kh: 
ḍāmaropāyikā] sakalasattvottāraṇahetostrailokyavajraracitā [g: ttvoddhā ; kh: 
caritā] samāptā ||

267.

namo [ṭhis sādhana is to be found in k, b only. ṣee folia 40 et. seq.] 
bhūtaḍāmarāya |

prajñopāyamayaṃ krodhaṃ bodhicittasamudbhavam |
praṇamyārādhanaṃ vakṣye bhūtaḍāmarasaṃjñakam ||

tatra bhāvasādhakaḥ praviṣṭamaṇḍalaḥ sarvasamayābhiṣikto'nujñātaśca kvacit 
girigahvarādau mano'nukūle pradeśe paṭādigatamūrtiṃ bhagavantaṃ 
dhūpādibāhyādhyātmikapūjābhiḥ sampūjya pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt |

ratnatrayaṃ me śaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ pratidiśāmyagham |
anumode jagatpuṇyaṃ buddhabodhau dadhe manaḥ ||

iti | hastadvaye akāreṇa candramaṇḍalaṃ tadupari jvalathūṁkāraṃ dhyātvā 
sampuṭāñjaliṃ kurvannidaṃ vadet oṃ siddhavajra hūṁ | hrīḥkāreṇa kaṇṭhe'ṣṭadalaṃ 
padmaṃ jihvādale hūṁkāreṇa śubhavajraṃ vajrādijihvetyadhitiṣṭhet | sthānarakṣāṃ 
vidadhyāt anena vidhinā | tatrāyaṃ vidhiḥ - hūṁkāramuccārayan jhaṭiti 
krodharājamātmānaṃ niṣpādya 
raktasūtrāvabaddharaktapuṣpamālādivibhūṣitānayonayādikīlakān hūṁkāraniṣpannān 
prāvṛṇmeghanibhān nābhideśādadhaḥ śūlākārānūrddhvakrodhākṛtīn adho 
vighnān tīkṣṇakīlena mantramuccārayan oṃ gha gha ghātaya ghātaya sarvaduṣṭān kīlaya 
kīlaya sarvapāpān phaṭ hūṁ hūṁ hūṁ vajradhara ājñāpayati sarvaduṣṭakāyavākcittaṃ 
kīlaya hūṁ phaṭ |

p. 526)

āḥkārajaṃ ca tasyāgre ravibimbakarojjvalam |
dikṣu sarvāsu kīlāni raśmijvālāni cintayet ||

taiśca paramparāvabaddhapañjarāntargataṃ svahṛdaye aṁkāreṇa sūryaṃ tatropari 
kalpāgniriva raṁkāramiti vinyasya anyonyāṅguṣṭhaṃ praveṣṭavyaṃ tarjanyau dvau 
prasārya lalāṭādārabhya pāpāsphoṭayogena sphoṭayet | oṃ hana vidhvaṃsaya nāśaya 
pāpān hūṁ phaṭ ityanena sandahya ātmānaṃ bāhyādhyātmikavikalpapāpāśca sphāṭya 
grāhyagrāhakavinirmuktaṃ gaganamiva cintayet | tato - 

vidhūtakalpanācittaṃ svasaṃvedyaṃ prabhāsvaram |
hṛtkṛtānandahūṁkāraṃ hokārābhyāṃ vidarbhitam ||

madadhātusamākrāntaṃ cittamātraprabhāvitam |
svacittenaikatāṃ kuryāt tīkṣṇaiḥ kiraṇasañcayaiḥ ||

pratibimbavat arddhendusaṃyuktaṃ paśyet | tata akārādisvarādīn ravimaṇḍale nyasya 
hrasvasvarān pāpakān dīrghān prajñātmakān niścārya buddhavikurvitān kṛtvā 
punasteṣāṃ svararaśmiṣu svenaiva viśataḥ kalāyogena vyavasthitān cintayet | kakārādīn 
paścāt tenaiva nyāyena ṅaṇanamavarjitāṃstathaiva bodhipakṣān vicintayediti | tataḥ 
pañcavarṇakān buddhān saṃsphārya praveśyānekagarbhastimitaṃ sitapadmaṃ paśyet | 
tasyopari śrīkrodharājaḥ hūṁ ityuccārya jvālākṛṣṭaṃ ātmānaṃ bhāvayet | 
krodhāveśaḥ - tataḥ krodhavajro'hamityahaṅkāraṃ kuryāt | tatkulaṃ punaḥ 
niścārya praveśya śrībhūtaḍāmaro'hamiti niścayamutpādya oṃ vajrāveśaya pātaya 
hūṁ | krodhāveśena ca mantrayukto vajrāveśaḥ | tata ātmānaṃ 

p. 527)

vajrakrodhaṃ niścitya samayo'haṃ samayamudrāṃ baddhvā samayasattvaṃ tiṣṭhasva 
māmiti vadet | 

anāmikādvayaṃ veṣṭya tarjanīdvayaṃ kuñcayet |
kaniṣṭhāṃ madhyamāṃ caiva jyeṣṭhāṅguṣṭhena [ḍ, dh: reads as 
jyeṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakrameṇa] cākramet ||

asya mantraḥ - oṃ vajra phaṭ | hṛdūrṇāyāṃ gale mūrdhni pratiṣṭhāpyādhitiṣṭhet | 
tataḥ ṣaḍaṅgavinyāsaṃ kuryāt tanmudrāmantraiḥ | anyonyamuṣṭiṃ kṛtvā 
madhyamāṅguliṃ prasārayet śiromudrā | mantraḥ oṃ hanavajra hūṁ śiraḥ | asyā eva 
madhyamāṅgulyoḥ praveśaṃ kṛtvā śikhāmudrā | mantraḥ oṃ dahavajra hūṁ śikhā 
| asyā eva mudrāyā aṅguṣṭhau pārśvataḥ dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇe vāmaṃ vāme netramudrā 
| mantraḥ dīptavajra hūṁ netra | anyonyamuṣṭiṃ kṛtvā kaniṣṭhikādvayaṃ veṣṭayet | 
tarjanīṃ prasārya hṛdayamudrā | hūṁkārādhiṣṭhitaṃ krodhavajraṃ hṛdaye dhyātvā 
oṃ vajraroṣa hūṁ hṛdayam | asyā eva mudrāyā tarjanīṃ kuṇḍalīṃ kṛtvā 
kavacamudrā | mantraḥ oṃ dṛḍhavajra hūṁ kavaca | asyā eva mudrāyāḥ tarjanīdvayaṃ 
prasārya astramudrā | mantraḥ oṃ hana daha paca korhdavajra duṣṭān māraya māraya hūṁ 
phaṭ astramantraḥ | triruccārya hūṁ aḥ bruvan purataḥ sūryamaṇḍale nīlaṃ krodharājaṃ 
nyasya vajro'hamiti vāg bruvan parāvṛttitaḥ krodhakrodho'hamiti bhāvayet | tato 
bhagavantaṃ sarvadevatābhiḥ sahāvāhayet | anyonyāntaritaṃ kṛtvā tarjanīdvayaṃ 
kuñncitaṃ īṣaccālayedaṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ 

p. 528)

tarjanyāvākarṣayet | oṃ vajramahākrodha samayamanupālaya bhagavan śīghramāgaccha 
sarvadevatābhiḥ saha hrīḥ jaḥ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā |

namo yamāntakāya |

ādau tāvat sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ sādhako raktākāraniṣpannaṃ [ḍh: ṅkā]

raktayamāriṃ vakṣyamāṇākṛtiṃ [kh, ḍh, k, b: omit]

śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'hamiti | tadanantaraṃ rakta##-

[k, b: sthite pade] ekamukhaṃ dvibhujaṃ vāmakareṇa raktaparipūrṇakapālaṃ 
dakṣiṇakare muṇḍāṅkitasitadaṇḍadharaṃ sarpābharaṇabhūṣitaṃ [k: sarvā]

p. 529)

sārdramuṇḍāṅkitaṃ sitadaṇḍaṃ niścalaṃ vibhāvya tanmadhye

guhyavajraṃ hūṁkārapariṇataṃ bhagavatyā guhyāṣṭadalakamalaṃ rakta-āḥkāreṇa |

hūṁ hṛdayamantraḥ |

269.

tathaiva pāpadeśanādipūrvakaṃ śūnyatāṃ vicintya rakta-ākārajasūrye 
raktahūṁkāraṃ tatpariṇataṃ sārdrapītamuṇḍāṅkitasitadaṇḍaṃ hūṁkārādhiṣṭhitaṃ

raktaparipūrṇakapālavāmakaraṃ sārdrapītamuṇḍāṅkitasitadaṇḍadakṣiṇakaraṃ

akṣobhyamukuṭinaṃ svābhaprajñāliṅgitaṃ [k: svāhā] mahiṣopari

bhagavatyāśca āḥkārapariṇataṃ padmaṃ vibhāvya oṃ sarvatathāgatānurāgeṇa 
vajrasvabhāvātmako'hamityadhitiṣṭhet | tadanu nābheradhaḥ sadraktāṣṭadalakamalopari 
[ḍh: vamadra ; k, g: ṇataṃ] raktahrīṁkāraṃ jvalantaṃ cintayet | tataḥ vuṁ 
āṁ jiṁ khaṁ hūṁ iti mantrākṣaraiḥ samayadravyasevāpūrvakaṃ dvitricatuḥsandhyaṃ

svāhā | oṃ yamāntaka hūṁ |

|| āryaraktayamārisādhanaṃ samāptam ||

270.

oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā | oṃ yamāntakṛt [k,

tanmadhye raktahūṁkāraṃ tatsarvaṃ [k, g: tameva] pariṇamya

p. 532)

vajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | tadanu nābheradhastāt raktāṣṭadalakamalopari [k, g: 
kāṣṭhada] raktahrīḥkāraṃ jvalantaṃ cintayet | bhagavānekamukho dvibhujaḥ 
vāmakareṇa raktapūrṇakapālaṃ dakṣiṇe muṇḍāṅkitasitadaṇḍadharaṃ mahiṣopari 
viśvakamalasthaṃ bhāvayet prajñā tu madavihvalā galadaṃśukā dvibhujaikamukhī 
bhagavatā saha sampuṭayogenāvasthitā | evambhūtaṃ yamāriṃ bhāvayet | trisandhyaṃ

[k, g: omit ḍāka] imaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇantu samayaṃ rakṣantu sarvasiddhiṃ 
prayacchantu yathaivaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ bhuñjatha pibatha mātikramatha sarvasiddhaye 
sahāyakā bhavatha hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā - sārvabhautikabalimantraḥ |

vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ [k, g: omit the second phaṭ] devadattasya 
śāntiṃ kuru namaḥ svāhā | puṣṭau vauṣaṭ ākṛṣṭau jaḥ vuṁ mohe hoḥ vaśye laṁ 
stambhe hūṁ phaṭ preraṇe hūṁ nigrahe aparābhyāmuccāṭanam | evaṃ stambhādīni 
karmaprasarāṇi vidarbhayet |

śāntike rocanayā rakṣā bhavati | sitanetrakarpaṭe bhūrjavaṃśatvacavidhautakarpaṭe 
[k, ḍh: karpaṭabhuje] vā cakradvayamabhilikhya [k, g; dvayaṃ 
likhya] namaskāravidarbhitaṃ akālamūlādirahite śarāvasampuṭe'vasthāpya 
ghṛtamadhumadhye prakṣipya sitasūtreṇa veṣṭayitvā trisandhyaṃ śuklapuṣpeṇārcayitvā

p. 533)

pūrvābhimukhaṃ sitavarṇaṃ yamāntakarūpaṃ dhyātvā purataścandramaṇḍalopaviṣṭaṃ 
sādhyaṃ dṛṣṭvā sitakalaśaiḥ paṃcāmṛtaparipūrṇairabhiṣiñcayantaṃ dhyātvā 
mantraṃ japet oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana devadattasya śāntiṃ kuru namaḥ svāhā |

śāntikavidhiḥ |

puruṣāṇāṃ śāntike pauṣṭike vā kuṅkumena cakradvayamabhilikhya vauṣaṭkārasahitaṃ 
ghṛtamadhumadhye prakṣipya śarāvasampuṭamadhye'vasthāpya pītasūtreṇa veṣṭayitvā 
trisandhyaṃ pītapuṣpairarcayitvā uttarābhimukhaṃ pītavarṇāṃ 
yamāntakamūrtimavalambya pītavarṇacaturmaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ pītakanakakalaśaiḥ 
pītavarṇāmṛtairabhiṣiñcayantaṃ [k, g: varṇasūtrai] dhyātvā mantraṃ 
japet oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ devadattasya puṣṭiṃ kuru vauṣaṭ 
svāhā |

pauṣṭikavidhiḥ |

bhūrje karpaṭe vā alaktakaraktacandanenānāmikāraktamiśreṇa cakradvayamabhilikhya 
hoḥkāranāmasahitaṃ vidarbhya suparipakvalohitaśarāvasampuṭe'vasthāpya 
ghṛtamadhumadhye prakṣiyāraktapuṣpairabhyarcya paścimābhimukhaṃ 
raktayamārirūpamātmānaṃ dhyātvā raktacandramaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ sādhyaṃ vicintya 
svakāyanirgataraśminābhiṣicya vihvalībhūtaṃ pādayornipatitaṃ vicintya mantraṃ japet oṃ 
hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ devadattasya kṛte [Omitted in k, 
g] yajñadattaṃ vaśaṃ kuru hoḥ [g: omits vaśaṃ kuru hoḥ svāhā] svāhā | 
yadi vaśaṃ na gacchati tadā ghṛtamadhurahitaṃ tameva cakraṃ [k, g: add 
vahninā] 

p. 534)

nirdhūmāṅgāreṇa tāpayet oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ 
devadattasya kṛte yajñadattaṃ vaśaṃ kuru hoḥ iti mantraṃ japet |

vaśyavidhiḥ |

śmaśānakarpaṭe rajasvalākarpaṭe vā lākṣārasamiśreṇa cakradvayamabhilikhya 
jaḥkārahrīḥkāreṇa nāmākṣarasahitena vidarbhya strīkapālasampuṭe'vasthāpya 
[k, g: trika] raktapuṣpairabhyarcya sandhyārkanibhaṃ 
yamāntakarūpamātmānaṃ raktavarṇāṅkuśapāśadhāriṇaṃ dhyātvā tasya hṛdayaṃ 
aṅkuśena viddhvā pāśena galake baddhvā āgacchantaṃ cintayet | yadi nāgacchati tadā 
khadirāgniṣu tāpayet mantraṃ japet | iti karmaprasarāṇi tantre jñātavyāni |

|| raktayamārisādhanaṃ samāptam ||

namaḥ kṛṣṇayamāraye |

trimaṇḍala trivajrāgra ghoṣavajra namo'stu te ||

vairocana mahāśuddha vajraśānta mahārate |
prakṛtiprabhāsvarāgrāgra dveṣavajra namo'stu te ||

ratnarāja sugāmbhīrya khavajrākāśanirmala |
svabhāvaśuddha nirlepa kāmavajra namo'stu te ||

vajrāmita mahārāga nirvikalpa khavajradhṛk |
rāgapāramitāprāpta bhāṣavajra namo'stu te ||

amogha vajrasambhūta sarvāśāparipūraka |
śuddhasvabhāvasambhūta vajrasattva namo'stu te ||

p. 546)

anilānalacakrasaṃsthaṃ dhyātvā pañcāmṛtaṃ kapālāntaḥ |
praṇavopetaṃ sārccistryakṣarajaptena tenaiva ||

prīṇayet svaṃ parāṃścaiva tato mantraṃ sphuran japet |
prapūjya saṃharet cānte svayogātmyaṃ visarjayet ||

iti racanāsaṅkalitaṃ śaśabhṛdvalayaṃ tu yacchubhaṃ tena |
maṅgalayukto loko labhatāṃ jainaṃ [k, ḍh: jinaṃ] paraṃ śaśvat ||

|| śrīmadyasārisādhanam ||

|| kṛtiḥ śrīmaṅgalasenasya ||

274.

namo yamāntakāya |

hūṁbījaṃ niṣpādya śuddhiparyantataḥ sūryasthahūṁkāraniṣpannaṃ yamāntakaṃ 
kruddhaṃ ūrdhvakeśaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ ṣaḍbhujaṃ ṣaṭcaraṇaṃ mahiṣārūḍhaṃ 
pratyālīḍhasthitaṃ naramuṇḍaruṇḍairvibhūṣitaṃ atibhayānakākāraṃ 
vyāghracarmanivasanaṃ dakṣiṇe khaḍgamudgaravajrāṇi vāme ghaṇṭāvajrapāśamuṣalān 
dhārayantaṃ mukuṭe akṣobhyaṃ vibhāvayet | tataḥ samayamudrāṃ bandhayet | 
sampuṭāñjaliṃ baddhvā aṅguṣṭhau vikasitau tarjanyormadhyayugatṛtīyaparvāṇi 
bhañjayet | oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā iti mantraṃ japet |

|| kṛṣṇayamārisādhanam ||

p. 547)

275.

ādau tāvanmantrī [k: omits] sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ sādhako raktākāraniṣpannaṃ 
[ḍh: ṅkāraṃ] sūryamaṇḍalaṃ svahṛdi vibhāvya tadupari nīlahūṁkāraṃ 
vinyasya hūṁkāraraśmijayamāntakādīn gurubuddhabodhisattvāṃścāvalambya 
[k, g: buddhasattvā] puṣpādibhiḥ sampūjya namaskṛtya 
triśaraṇagamanādikaṃ kṛtvā śūnyatāṃ bhāvayet oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | tadanantaraṃ raktāṁkārajasūryamaṇḍalopari 
hūṁkāraṃ tatpariṇataṃ vajrāṅkitanīladaṇḍaṃ hūṁkārādhiṣṭhitaṃ vibhāvayet 
hūṁkāraraśmicoditasarvatathāgatapraveśapūrvakam | etat sarvaṃ pariṇamya yamāriṃ 
vicintayedātmānaṃ pratyālīḍhapadasthitaṃ ekamukhaṃ dvibhujaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ 
dakṣiṇakare vajrāṅkitodyatanīladaṇḍaṃ vāmakare tarjanīpāśaṃ hṛdi evambhūtaṃ 
yamāriṃ sphuratsaṃharadrūpaṃ viśvadalakamalopari sūryasthamahiṣārūḍhaṃ bhāvayet | 
tato bhagavato hṛdi sūryamaṇḍale hūṁkārapariṇataṃ hūṁkārādhiṣṭhitaṃ 
nīladaṇḍaṃ hūṁkāranirgataraśminā jagadavabhāsya punastatraiva praveśayet | evaṃ 
krameṇa yāvadicchaṃ sphuraṇasaṃharaṇaṃ kuryāt samayādividhipūrvakam | evaṃ 
dvitricatuḥsandhyaṃ bhāvayet oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā iti 
mantraṃ japet |

|| kṛṣṇayamārisādhanaṃ samāptam ||

p. 548)

276.

antastuṣāramapi [kh: mahiṣeṇa] yena dayāvaśena sandarśitaṃ 
haṭhavineyajaneṣu raudram |

g: bhāṣa] yamārirūpam |

p. 549)

nīlāṅgamekavadanaṃ dvibhujaṃ saroṣaṃ savye [k: sarve] kare

[k, g: harita, kh: pheru] |

sarvam |

277.

śrīmantamantaḥkaruṇāmayaṃn [k, b: tanta, kh: mantra] taṃ 
sattvārthahetorbahirugrarūpam |

p. 551)

b: saṃjñikaṃ] |

savyodyatacārukhaḍgam ||

samantato nirgataraktabhābhistraidhātukaṃ vyāpya susaṃsthitāṃstān [kh: 
sthitātmān] |

p. 552)

krodhāṃśca vidyājinabodhisattvān sañcodya cānīya ca bhānubimbe ||

praveśya tasmin sahasaikalolībhūtvaiva [k, b: bhūtai] jātaṃ punareva 
sūrye |
sphuranmayūkhairatibhāsvaraṃ [k, ḍh, g, kh: bhāsu] ca raktaṃ 
suvistīrṇataraṃ [k: viśīrṇa] ca tatra ||

ṣaḍgatisthān ||

praveśya |

calavajrikaṃ ca] lambajihvam |
āpiṅgaśmaśūrddhvagamūrddhajātaṃ babhrubhruvaṃ raktatarākṣayugmam ||

p. 553)

kṛṣṇaṃ saroṣaṃ bhṛkuṭīlalāṭaṃ hutkārināgāṣṭakabhūṣitāṅgam 
[k, b: sphu, kh: hṛ] |

278.

ādau tāvanmantrī karuṇāvān sukhāsanāsīnaḥ purato bhagavantaṃ yamadviṣaṃ 
paṭādigatamavalambya gurubuddhabodhisattvāṃśca dṛṣṭvā nānāpūjābhiḥ sampūjya 
praṇamya ca pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | tadanantaraṃ svabhāvaśuddhetyādimantreṇa 
svabhāvaśuddhimadhimucya sarvadharmanairātmyaṃ vibhāvya nijamantramuccārayet oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | tataḥ svahṛdaye akārajacandramaṇḍale 
nīlahūṁkāraṃ kvacit sūryasthaṃ hūṁkāraṃ dṛṣṭvā tadraśmibhirvighnān vimarddya 
yamāntakākārakrodhāṃśca sattvārthakaraṇapravaṇān saṃsphārya jhaṭiti tatpariṇāmena 
yamāntakamātmānaṃ vicintayet kṛṣṇaṃ trimukhaṃ ṣaḍbhujaṃ kvacit ṣaṇmukhaṃ 
ṣaṭcaraṇaṃ ca daṃṣṭrākarālavadanamāraktanayanaṃ [k, g, kh, a: 
māraktavadanamārakta] cakrakhaḍgamūṣaladharadakṣiṇabhujatrayaṃ [k, b: 
vajra0]  vettālīparaśupāśadhārivāmabhujatrayaṃ 
khaḍgamudgaravajradharadakṣiṇakaraṃ vāme ghaṇṭāvajrapāśamūṣalāni kvacit 
ūrddhvapiṅgalakeśamuṇḍamālāvibhūṣitamaulina maṣṭanāgendraviracitābharaṇaṃ 
akṣobhyamukuṭinaṃ viśvadalakamalopari sūryamaṇḍale 
pratyālīḍhapadasthitamāraktaprabhāmaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvya stanāntaracandramaṇḍale 
hūṁkāraṃ paśyet | kāyavākcittamaṇḍaleṣu oṃ āḥ hūṁ ityakṣaratrayaṃ 
sitaraktakṛṣṇaṃ yathākrameṇa paśyet | tadanantaraṃ 
svahṛdbījanirgatadevībhirātmānaṃ [k, ḍh, g: devatībhi] sampūjya 
[k, b: saṃyojya] saṃstutya ca mantraṃ paṭhet oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana 
hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā |

|| kṛṣṇayamārisādhanam ||

279. [ṭhis sādhana is twice given in the ḍ, dh, dh. k, ḍh and g]

oṃ namo yamāntakāya |

kapālamālinaṃ [k, b: mālaṃ cīraṃ] vīraṃ nāgādyābharaṇotkaṭam |
natvā yamāntakaṃ nāthaṃ likhyate tasya sādhanam ||

sukhāsane samāsīno hṛnnyastanīlahūṁkṛtiḥ |
tajjapūjāviśeṣaiśca vibhumagre [k, b: sampūjya bhaktito vibhūṃ] 
prapūjayet [k, g: pūjan japet] ||

kṛtvā tasya [k, b: tataḥ] purodeśe pāpādideśanādikam [k, 
ḍh, g, kh: durita] |
niḥsvabhāvaṃ jagat sarvaṃ jhaṭityālokya sarvataḥ ||

hṛdbījodbhavacihnotthaṃ pratyālīḍhāṅghripaṅkajam |
bhṛkuṭībhaṅgabhīmāsyaṃ piṅgalaśmaśrumūrddhajam ||

tribhirvaktrairlalajjihvairbhīmadaṃṣṭrākarālinam [k, b: libhiḥ] |
raktākṣaṃ ca bṛhatkukṣiṃ navanīlaghanadyutim ||

mārttaṇḍamaṇḍalasthaṃ ca śirasyasthivibhūṣitam |
vyāghracarmāṃśukaṃ kharvaṃ [kh: khaḍgaṃ] sarvagarvāpahāriṇam ||

sūryaprabhāmahātejaḥpūritāśeṣadiṅmukham |
jvalajjvālāvalītīvrayugāntavahnisannibham ||

cakrāsimūṣalaṃ bhāsvadvettālīparśupāśakam [kh: pāśaṃ] |
bibhrāṇaṃ ṣaṭsu divyeṣu savyāvasavyapāṇiṣu [k, ḍh, g, kh: 
savyogra0] ||

saroṣaṃ bhīmamatyugraṃ yamāriṃ māramarddanam |
hūṁkārāpūrṇakaṇṭhaṃ ca dhyāyād bhītivivarjitam ||

p. 556)

viṃśatyarddhasahasrāṇāṃ mantrāṇāṃ mantravit sadā |
japannavyagracittastu labhate siddhimīhitām ||

pūrvasevāvidheścaiṣa mantraḥ sidhyati mantriṇām |
karmānurūpato varṇaṃ noktamasyātivistarāt [k, b: smābhirvi0] ||

gurūpadeśatastacca [k, b: stattvaṃ] cintyaṃn vāñchitasiddhaye |
jñeyaṃ ca yatnato dhīraiḥ kravyān pañcāpi sevayet |
balyupahārayogena catuḥsandhyaṃ vidhīyatām [k, ḍh, kh: 
vidheyamiti] ||

iti jāpamantraḥ - oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ vikṛtānana hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā |

|| yamāntakasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

280.

namo yamāntakāya [k, b: and kh: give the namaskāra] |

prathamaṃ kṛtamukhaśaucādiko bhāvakaḥ svahṛdi akārajacandre kṛṣṇayaṁkāraṃ 
vibhāvya tatkiraṇairgurubuddhādīnānīya sampūjya pāpadeśanādikaṃ vidadhyāt 
[k, b: dhyāyāt] | tato yaṁkāramātrātmako yogī 
śūnyatāmāmukhīkṛtyādhiṣṭhāya ca praṇidhimanusmaret | tadanu rephodbhavasūrye 
hūṁkārabhūtavajramudgaraṃ ca svabījalāñchanaṃ vibhāvya tajjanitaṃ vajrabhūmiṃ 
vajraprākāraṃ vajrapañjaraṃ ca bhāvayet | tanmadhye candramaṇḍalaṃ 
dviguṇasvaravarṇamayadvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇodbhutaṃ prajñāsvabhāvaṃ 
samīkṣya 

p. 557)

tadupari aśītyanuvyañjanaviśuddhidviguṇita ḍa ḍha da dha ya [k, ḍh, g, 
k, b: ya ra la va] la yutakādivyañjanairdviguṇiarniṣpannamaparaṃ 
śaśimaṇḍalamupāyarūpaṃ sphuradanantadīdhitiṃ dhyāyāt | tatastadubhayaniṣpannasūrye 
[k, ḍh, g, k, b: stadupari] nīlahūṁyaṁsamudbhūtaṃ 
navavārivāhanīlaṃ mahodaraṃ piṅgalatarabhrūśmaśrūrddhvakeśaṃ daṃṣṭrotkaṭaṃ 
kharvapīnamahiṣasthaṃ vyāghrājinadharaṃ mṛgapaticarmottarīyaṃ atibhayapradaṃ 
svaśarīraraśminirmitapañcakrodhakṛtasphuraṇaṃ ābharaṇīkṛtaphaṇirājaṃ 
vigalacchatārddhanaraśiraḥśreṇīdharaṃ [k, ḍh, g: omit nara] 
ṣaḍbhujaṃ savyabhujairmudgarakaravālakuliśadharaṃ [k, b: śaraṃ] 
vāmaistribhistarjanikāpāśamūṣaladharaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ prathamaṃ corddhvaṃ [k, 
b: corddhvanīlaṃ] ca nīlaṃ sitapītaraktakṛṣṇaṃ savyetarayormukhaṃ kramaśaḥ 
sarvāṇi mukhāni raktavartulatrinetrāṇi ṣaṭcararāṃ ca padābhyāṃ pratyālīḍhaṃ 
dvābhyāṃ [ḍh, g, kh: tābhyāṃ] viracitanṛtyaṃ aparābhyāṃ 
kṛtasattvaparyaṅkaṃ sphuradarkamadhyasaṃsthān vighnānāmarddayantaṃ 
aṣṭakaroṭīsughaṭitabhīṣaṇataramuṇḍamālām | tadanu kṣiṁ [kh: khiṁ] jaṁ 
khaṁ laṁ skaṃṁ saṁbījasambhūtāḥ 
kṣitigarbhavajrapāṇikhagarbhalokeśvaraviskambhisamastabhadranāmānaścakṣuṣi karṇe 
ghrāṇe jihvāyāṃ stanayośca nābhau ca cakrāśaniratnāmbujasāyakaśatakoṭidharaṃ

mahāraudraṃ trikāyātmakaṃ pañcabbuddhamukuṭinaṃ 

p. 598)

naramuṇḍamālābharaṇaṃ bhavabhayaṅkaram | pūrve mahāmāyā maheśvarā 
siṃhākrāntā pratyālīḍhapadasthitā caturbhujā vāme kapālaṃ dvitīye ḍamarukaṃ 
vādayantī dakṣiṇe kartri dvitīye mudgaraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇā muktakeśā trilocanā 
vikaṭadaṃṣṭrā ca | dakṣiṇe yamadūtī nīlavarṇā caturbhujā dakṣiṇahastābhyāṃ 
padmadaṇḍakartridharā vāmakarābhyāṃ raktapūrṇakapālacāmaradhṛtavatī 
pratyālīḍhapadasthitā yamamahiṣākrāntā nagnā [k, g: magrā] muktakeśī | 
paścime kāladūtī vāme kapālagomuṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe mudgaratriśūlaṃ aśvārūḍhā 
pratyālīḍhapadā raktavarṇā [k: rṇādisthitā] muktakeśī trilocanī | sarvā devatyo 
vikaṭadantā nāgābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ | catuṣkoṇe caturdevyaḥ - pūrvadakṣiṇakoṇe 
kālikā kṛṣṇavarṇā dvibhujā kartrikapālahastā pratyālīḍhapadasthitā śavārūḍhā 
dakṣiṇa [ḍh, b: nairṛtye] paścimakoṇe carcikā raktavarṇā 
kartrikapālahastā pūrvavat sarvam paścimottarakoṇe [ḍh, b: vāyavye] 
canḍeśvarī pītavarṇā śavārūḍhā caṇḍamṛgahastā prayālīḍhapadasthitā 
uttarapūrvakoṇe [ḍh, b: īśāne] kuliśeśvarī śuklavarṇā vajradaṇḍahastā 
śavārūḍhā pratyālīḍhapadasthitā | sarvā devatyo nagnā vikaṭadantā trilocanā 
muktakeśinyaḥ | ebhiraṣṭayoginībhiḥ [k: ebhiḥ pari0] parivṛtaṃ 
śūnyatāsamādhisthasadvajrānandikaśavaṃ pratyālīḍhapadena vajrabhairavākrāntaṃ 
evambhūtaṃ bhagavantaṃ dhyātvā mantraṃ japet | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ - oṃ 
ṣoḍaśabhujāya hūṁhūṁ kili kili 

p. 599)

caṇḍaghoradaṃṣṭrotkaṭabhairavāya sarvasiddhidāyakāya hili hili kili kili he svāhā | etaṃ 
mantraṃ japet |

ayaṃ mantrarājaḥ satataṃ japena buddhatvamihaiva [k: miha vajramaṇi] janmani 
dadāti jātismaratvamayutajāpena aṣṭau mahāsiddhayaḥ sidhyanti | atha baliṃ dātumicchati 
tadaiva bhagavantaṃ saparivāraṃ purato bhāvya balimantreṇāṣṭau baliṃ dadyāt | tatrāyaṃ 
balimantraḥ - oṃ namo mahākālāya mahādaṃṣṭro tatkaṭabhairavāya 
mahākrodhādhipataye piṅgorddhvakeśāya ṣoḍaśabhujāya caturviṃśatinetrāya oṃ turu 
turu kha kha khāhi khāhi idaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa hahā hihī huhū hehai hohau 
[k, g: omit ho hau] haṃhaḥ mahāvajrabuddhāya kāye māṃ adhitiṣṭha tiṣṭha 
ṭaṭa ṭaṭa maṭa maṭa kaṭa kaṭa kāraya kāraya garjaya garjaya bhīmamahākrodhādhipataye 
nāśaya nāśaya sarvaśatrūn duṣṭasattvadamaka pālaya pālaya māṃ dehi dehi sarvān 
nāśaya nāśaya hūṁ hai kili kili bhaḥ hūṁ hūṁ sarvayakṣapiśācāśca 
rākṣasakinnarāśca sarvaśāntiṃ kuru kuru haṃ haḥ nāśaya nāśaya mahākālāya 
kālarūpāya [k, ḍh, b: omit kāla0] mahābhayānakāya bhītaṃ dama dama 
damaya damaya nāśanaṃ sarvaduṣṭasattvānuddharata nāgādhipataye tāraya tāraya 
nāśaya nāśaya tāraya tāraya haḥ haḥ kaṭaḥ kaṭaḥ khaṭaḥ khaṭaḥ phaṭaḥ phaṭaḥ 
cchinda cchinda hana hana daha daha paca paca matha matha nāśaya nāśaya ghaṭa ghaṭa
